G_{12}=\frac{2}{\pi i}\int\frac{(\bar{f}(\alpha)-\bar{f}(\beta))(\partial_{\alpha}^{s}D_{t}\bar{\zeta}(\alpha)-\partial_{\beta}^{s}D_{t}\bar{\zeta}(\beta)}{(\zeta(\alpha)-\zeta(\beta))^{2}}\partial_{\beta}D_{t}\zeta d\beta,

G_{21}=\frac{1}{\pi i}\int\frac{(D_{t}\zeta(\alpha)-D_{t}\zeta(\beta))(\partial_{\alpha}^{s}D_{t}\zeta-\partial_{\beta}^{s}D_{t}\zeta)}{(\zeta(\alpha)-\zeta(\beta))^{2}}(\zeta_{\beta}-1)d\beta,

G_{22}=\frac{1}{\pi i}\int\frac{(D_{t}\zeta(\alpha)-D_{t}\zeta(\beta))(\partial_{\alpha}^{s}D_{t}\zeta-\partial_{\beta}^{s}D_{t}\zeta)}{(\zeta(\alpha)-\zeta(\beta))^{2}}(\bar{\zeta}_{\beta}-1)d\beta.

We divide the rest of the proof into two parts:

(1)

We prove that

G_{21}=O(\epsilon^{4}t^{-1-\delta})_{L^{2}},

G_{22}=\frac{4\alpha}{it}\partial_{\alpha}^{s}D_{t}\zeta|\partial_{\alpha}D_{t}\zeta|^{2}+O(\epsilon^{3}t^{-1-\delta})_{L^{2}},

and

(5.21)

\frac{2}{A}D_{t}\bar{\theta}_{s}G_{2}=\frac{16\alpha}{iAt}|D_{t}^{2}\zeta|^{2}|\partial_{\alpha}^{s}D_{t}\zeta|^{2}+O(\epsilon^{3}t^{-1-\delta}\mathcal{E}_{s})_{L^{1}},

for certain
\delta>0
. And thus

Re\int\frac{2}{A}D_{t}\bar{\theta}G_{2}=O(\epsilon^{3}t^{-1-\delta}\mathcal{E}_{s}).

Subcase:
For
G_{21}

Note that it suffices to consider

\left\|\overline{\frac{1}{\pi i}\int\frac{(D_{t}\zeta(\alpha)-D_{t}\zeta(\beta))(\partial_{\alpha}^{s}D_{t}\zeta-\partial_{\beta}^{s}D_{t}\zeta)}{(\zeta(\alpha)-\zeta(\beta))^{2}}(\zeta_{\beta}-1)d\beta}\right\|_{L^{2}}.

Note that
D_{t}\bar{\zeta},(\bar{\zeta}_{\alpha}-1)/\zeta_{\alpha}
,
(I+\mathcal{H})\partial_{\alpha}^{s}D_{t}\bar{\zeta}
are holomorphic in
\Omega(t)
and we write

\displaystyle\overline{\frac{\partial_{\alpha}^{s}D_{t}\zeta-\partial_{\beta}^{s}D_{t}\zeta}{(\zeta(\alpha)-\zeta(\beta)^{2}}(\zeta_{\beta}-1)}

\displaystyle=\frac{\partial_{\alpha}^{s}D_{t}\bar{\zeta}-\partial_{\beta}^{s}D_{t}\bar{\zeta}}{(\zeta(\alpha)-\zeta(\beta)^{2}}(\bar{\zeta}_{\beta}-1)

\displaystyle\ \ \ +(\partial_{\alpha}^{s}D_{t}\bar{\zeta}-\partial_{\beta}^{s}D_{t}\bar{\zeta})\frac{4iRe\{\zeta(\alpha)-\zeta(\beta)\}Im\{\zeta(\alpha)-\zeta(\beta)\}}{|\zeta(\alpha)-\zeta(\beta)|^{4}}(\bar{\zeta}_{\beta}-1).

Observe that

\displaystyle\text{Im}\{\zeta(\alpha)-\zeta(\beta)\}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2i}(\zeta(\alpha)-\zeta(\beta)-\overline{\zeta(\alpha)-\zeta(\beta)}),

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2i}\left\{[\zeta(\alpha)-\alpha-(\zeta(\beta)-\beta)]-\overline{[\zeta(\alpha)-\alpha-(\zeta(\beta)-\beta)]}\right\}

and thus, by Lemma
A.3
,

\displaystyle\left\lVert\int(D_{t}\bar{\zeta}(\alpha)-D_{t}\bar{\zeta}(\beta))(\partial_{\alpha}^{s}D_{t}\bar{\zeta}-\partial_{\beta}^{s}D_{t}\bar{\zeta})\frac{4iRe\left\{\zeta(\alpha)-\zeta(\beta)\right\}Im\left\{\zeta(\alpha)-\zeta(\beta)\right\}}{|\zeta(\alpha)-\zeta(\beta)|^{4}}(\bar{\zeta}_{\beta}-1)d\beta\right\rVert_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\ C\left\lVert\partial_{\alpha}^{s}D_{t}\zeta\right\rVert_{L^{2}}\left\lVert\partial_{\alpha}D_{t}\zeta\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\left\lVert\zeta_{\alpha}\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\left\lVert\zeta_{\alpha}-1\right\rVert_{W^{1,\infty}}^{2}\ln t,

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\ C\epsilon^{4}t^{-\frac{3}{2}}\ln t.

Hence we focus on the first term. Claim that

\left\lVert(I-\mathcal{H})\partial_{\alpha}^{s}D_{t}\bar{\zeta}\right\rVert_{L^{2}}\leq C\epsilon^{2}t^{-\frac{1}{2}}.

Actually, since
(I-\mathcal{H})D_{t}\bar{\zeta}=0
, there holds

\left\lVert(I-\mathcal{H})\partial_{\alpha}^{s}D_{t}\bar{\zeta}\right\rVert_{L^{2}}=\left\lVert[\partial_{\alpha}^{s},\mathcal{H}]D_{t}\bar{\zeta}\right\rVert_{L^{2}}\leq C\epsilon^{2}t^{-\frac{1}{2}}

as in the proof of Lemma
5.2
. Now we write

\displaystyle\int(D_{t}\bar{\zeta}(\alpha)-D_{t}\bar{\zeta}(\beta))\frac{\partial_{\alpha}^{s}D_{t}\bar{\zeta}-\partial_{\beta}^{s}D_{t}\bar{\zeta}}{(\zeta(\alpha)-\zeta(\beta)^{2}}(\bar{\zeta}_{\beta}-1)d\beta

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\ \int\frac{I+\mathcal{H}}{2}\left(\partial_{\alpha}^{s}D_{t}\bar{\zeta}-\partial_{\beta}^{s}D_{t}\bar{\zeta}\right)\frac{D_{t}\bar{\zeta}(\alpha)-D_{t}\bar{\zeta}(\beta)}{(\zeta(\alpha)-\zeta(\beta)^{2}}\frac{\bar{\zeta}_{\beta}-1}{\zeta_{\beta}}d\beta

\displaystyle+\int\frac{I-\mathcal{H}}{2}\left(\partial_{\alpha}^{s}D_{t}\bar{\zeta}-\partial_{\beta}^{s}D_{t}\bar{\zeta}\right)\frac{D_{t}\bar{\zeta}(\alpha)-D_{t}\bar{\zeta}(\beta)}{(\zeta(\alpha)-\zeta(\beta)^{2}}\frac{\bar{\zeta}_{\beta}-1}{\zeta_{\beta}}d\beta

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\ 0+(\epsilon^{4}t^{-\frac{3}{2}}\ln t)_{L^{2}}.

The first integral on the right hand side of the equality is zero due to the Cauchy integral Theorem. Therefore, we conclude that

\left\lVert G_{21}\right\rVert_{L^{2}}\leq C\epsilon^{4}t^{-\frac{3}{2}}\ln t.

Subcase:
For
G_{22}
:

We introduce the frequency decomposition:
f_{L}=P_{<t^{-2}}f
and
f_{H}=P_{\geq t^{-2}}f
. Applying this frequency decomposition to
D_{t}\zeta
in
G_{22}
, it is straightforward to verify (one should use Lemma
B.1
to obtain
\left\lVert\partial_{\alpha}(D_{t}\zeta)_{L}\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\leq C\epsilon t^{-1}
) that any term containing at least one low-frequency factor decays faster than
t^{-1}
.

Thus, it’s enough to consider

(5.22)

\tilde{G}_{22}=\frac{1}{\pi i}\int\frac{((D_{t}\zeta)_{H}(\alpha)-(D_{t}\zeta)_{H}(\beta))(\partial_{\alpha}^{s}D_{t}\zeta-\partial_{\beta}^{s}D_{t}\zeta)}{(\zeta(\alpha)-\zeta(\beta))^{2}}(\bar{\zeta}_{\beta}-1)d\beta

and we shall study it by considering the position of
|\alpha|
. Let
\mu>0
be a small constant.

Subsubcase:

|\alpha|
is far from
t
.

\bullet
For
|\alpha|<t^{1-\mu}
:

Note that by Lemma
4.3
and (
4.9
),

\partial_{\alpha}(D_{t}\zeta)_{H}=\frac{4\alpha^{2}}{it^{2}}\partial_{\alpha}^{2}(D_{t}\zeta)_{H}-\frac{4\alpha}{it^{2}}L_{0}\partial_{\alpha}(D_{t}\zeta)_{H}+\frac{2}{it}\Omega_{0}\partial_{\alpha}(D_{t}\zeta)_{H}=\frac{4\alpha^{2}}{it^{2}}\partial_{\alpha}^{2}(D_{t}\zeta)_{H}+O(\epsilon t^{-1+\delta_{0}}\ln t).

This implies that

\left\lVert\partial_{\alpha}(D_{t}\zeta)_{H}\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(|\alpha|<Ct^{1-\mu})}\leq C\epsilon t^{-\frac{1}{2}-2\mu},

and by Lemma
5.3
, we obtain

\displaystyle\left\lVert\tilde{G}_{22}\right\rVert_{L^{2}(|\alpha|<t^{1-\mu})}

\displaystyle\leq C\left(\left\lVert\partial_{\alpha}(D_{t}\zeta)_{H}\right\rVert_{W^{1,\infty}(|\alpha|\lesssim t^{1-\mu})}+\epsilon t^{-1/2}\left\lVert\partial_{\alpha}D_{t}\zeta\right\rVert_{W^{1,\infty}}\right)

\displaystyle\ \ \ \times\left\lVert\partial_{\alpha}^{s}D_{t}\zeta\right\rVert_{L^{2}}\left\lVert\zeta_{\alpha}-1\right\rVert_{W^{1,\infty}}\ln t+Ct^{-1+\mu}\left\lVert D_{t}\zeta\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\left\lVert\partial_{\alpha}^{s}D_{t}\zeta\right\rVert_{L^{2}}\left\lVert\zeta_{\alpha}-1\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\leq C\epsilon^{2}t^{-1-2\mu}\ln t\sqrt{\mathcal{E}_{s}}+C\epsilon^{3}t^{-1-\delta}.

\bullet
For
|\alpha|>t^{1+\mu}
:

We have

\displaystyle\partial_{\alpha}D_{t}\zeta=\partial_{t}(\zeta_{\alpha}-1)+O(\epsilon^{2}t^{-1})

\displaystyle=-\frac{it}{2\alpha}(\zeta_{\alpha}-1)+\frac{1}{\alpha}\Omega_{0}(\zeta_{\alpha}-1)+O(\epsilon^{2}t^{-1}),

\displaystyle=-\frac{it}{2\alpha}(\zeta_{\alpha}-1)+O(\epsilon t^{-1-\mu+\delta_{0}}),

which implies

\left\lVert\partial_{\alpha}D_{t}\zeta\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(|\alpha|>Ct^{1+\mu})}\leq C\epsilon t^{-\frac{1}{2}-\mu}.

Hence, applying Lemma
5.3
,

\displaystyle\left\lVert\tilde{G}_{22}\right\rVert_{L^{2}(|\alpha|>t^{1+\mu})}\leq

\displaystyle\ C\left(\left\lVert\partial_{\alpha}D_{t}\zeta\right\rVert_{W^{1,\infty}(|\alpha|\gtrsim t^{1+\mu})}+\epsilon t^{-1/2}\left\lVert\partial_{\alpha}D_{t}\zeta\right\rVert_{W^{1,\infty}}\right)

\displaystyle\ \ \ \times\left\lVert\partial_{\alpha}^{s}D_{t}\zeta\right\rVert_{L^{2}}\left\lVert\zeta_{\alpha}-1\right\rVert_{W^{1,\infty}}\ln t+Ct^{-1-\mu}\left\lVert D_{t}\zeta\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\left\lVert\partial_{\alpha}^{s}D_{t}\zeta\right\rVert_{L^{2}}\left\lVert\zeta_{\alpha}-1\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\ C\epsilon^{2}t^{-1-\mu}\ln t\sqrt{\mathcal{E}_{s}}+C\epsilon^{3}t^{-1-\delta}.

Subsubcase:

\alpha\in S(t)=\{\alpha\in\mathbb{R}\ |\ t^{1-\mu}\leq|\alpha|\leq t^{1+\mu}\}
:

Applying
f=P_{\geq t^{-2}}Q_{\alpha}
,
g=\partial_{\alpha}^{s}Q_{\alpha}
, and
h=\bar{\theta}_{\alpha}
to localization lemma
4.14
, together with Lemma
4.1
, we obtain:

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\pi i}1_{S(t)}\int\frac{((D_{t}\zeta)_{H}(\alpha)-(D_{t}\zeta)_{H}(\beta))(\partial_{\alpha}^{s}D_{t}\zeta-\partial_{\beta}^{s}D_{t}\zeta)}{(\zeta(\alpha)-\zeta(\beta))^{2}}(\bar{\zeta}_{\beta}-1)d\beta=

\displaystyle 2\frac{it^{2}}{4\alpha^{2}}1_{S(t)}(Q_{\alpha})_{H}\partial_{\alpha}^{s}Q_{\alpha}\partial_{\alpha}\bar{\theta}+e

where
\|e\|_{L^{2}}\leq C\epsilon^{3}t^{-\frac{5}{4}-\delta^{\prime}}
for some
\delta^{\prime}>0
. We use the formula

\partial_{\alpha}f=\frac{it^{2}}{4\alpha^{2}}f+\frac{1}{\alpha}L_{0}f-\frac{t}{2\alpha^{2}}\Omega_{0}f

and

\partial_{t}f=-\frac{it}{2\alpha}f+\frac{1}{\alpha}\Omega_{0}f=\frac{it}{2\alpha}f+\frac{1}{\alpha}\tilde{\Omega}_{0}f

(
f
represents an arbitrary function here)
to derive

\displaystyle\frac{it^{2}}{4\alpha^{2}}1_{S(t)}(Q_{\alpha})_{H}\partial_{\alpha}^{s}Q_{\alpha}\partial_{\alpha}\bar{\theta}

\displaystyle=\left(\frac{it^{2}}{4\alpha^{2}}\right)^{s}1_{S(t)}(Q_{\alpha})_{H}\partial_{\alpha}Q_{\alpha}\bar{\theta}_{\alpha}+O(\epsilon^{3}t^{-1-\delta})_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle=\left(\frac{it^{2}}{4\alpha^{2}}\right)^{s-1}\frac{t}{2\alpha}1_{S(t)}(Q_{\alpha})_{H}\partial_{\alpha}Q_{\alpha}\partial_{\alpha}D_{t}\bar{\theta}+O(\epsilon^{3}t^{-1-\delta})_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle=\frac{4\alpha^{2}}{it^{2}}\frac{t}{2\alpha}1_{S(t)}\partial_{\alpha}^{s}(Q_{\alpha})_{H}\partial_{\alpha}Q_{\alpha}\partial_{\alpha}D_{t}\bar{\theta}+O(\epsilon^{3}t^{-1-\delta})_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle=\frac{4\alpha}{it}\partial_{\alpha}^{s}D_{t}\zeta|\partial_{\alpha}D_{t}\zeta|^{2}+O(\epsilon^{3}t^{-1-\delta})_{L^{2}}.

Here we have used Lemma
4.8
and Lemma
4.1
. Also,

D_{t}\bar{\theta}_{s}=[D_{t},\partial_{\alpha}^{s}]\bar{\theta}+\partial_{\alpha}^{s}D_{t}\bar{\theta}=2\partial_{\alpha}^{s}D_{t}\bar{\zeta}+O(\epsilon^{2}t^{-\frac{1}{2}})_{L^{2}},

hence

\displaystyle\frac{2}{A}D_{t}\bar{\theta}_{s}\tilde{G}_{22}=\frac{16\alpha}{iAt}|\partial_{\alpha}^{s}D_{t}\bar{\zeta}|^{2}|\partial_{\alpha}D_{t}\zeta|^{2}+O(\epsilon^{4}t^{-1-\delta})_{L^{1}}.

Note that the first term is pure imaginary, which vanishes after taking the real part. Now we finally get

(5.23)

\left|Re\int\frac{2}{A}D_{t}\bar{\theta}_{s}G_{22}d\alpha\right|\leq C\epsilon^{3}t^{-1-\delta}\mathcal{E}_{s}+C\epsilon^{4}t^{-1-\delta}.

(2)

We aim to prove

(5.24)

(I-\mathcal{H})(G_{11}+G_{12})=O(\epsilon^{3}t^{-1-\delta})_{L^{2}}

for certain
\delta>0
. Once (
5.24
) is established, it directly follows that (note the trivial bound
\left\lVert G_{11}\right\rVert_{L^{2}}+\left\lVert G_{22}\right\rVert_{L^{2}}\leq C\epsilon^{3}t^{-1}\ln t
)

\displaystyle\left|\int D_{t}\bar{\theta}_{s}(G_{11}+G_{12})\right|

\displaystyle\leq\left|\int\partial_{\alpha}^{s}D_{t}\bar{\theta}G_{1}\right|+C\epsilon^{4}t^{-1-\delta}

\displaystyle\leq 2\left|\int\partial_{\alpha}^{s}D_{t}\bar{\zeta}(G_{11}+G_{12})\right|+C\epsilon^{4}t^{-1-\delta}

\displaystyle\leq 2\left|\int\frac{I+\mathcal{H}}{2}\partial_{\alpha}^{s}D_{t}\bar{\zeta}(G_{11}+G_{12})\right|+C\epsilon^{4}t^{-1-\delta}

\displaystyle=2\left|\int\frac{I+\mathcal{H}}{2}\partial_{\alpha}^{s}D_{t}\bar{\zeta}\frac{I-\mathcal{H}}{2}(G_{11}+G_{12})\right|+C\epsilon^{4}t^{-1-\delta}

\displaystyle\leq C\epsilon^{4}t^{-1-\delta}.

\bullet
Verification of (
5.24
).

Subcase:
For
G_{11}
:

Note that in this part,
f(\alpha)=\zeta-\alpha
.
Let us define the frequency decomposition as
f_{L}=P_{<t^{-1/2-\delta}}f,\ f_{H}=P_{\geq t^{-1/2-\delta}}f
for some

\left\lVert\partial_{\alpha}f_{L}\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\leq Ct^{-\frac{1}{2}-\delta}\left\lVert\Lambda f_{L}\right\rVert_{L^{2}}\leq C\epsilon t^{-\frac{1}{2}-\delta},

to handle terms involving the low-frequency component:

\displaystyle\left\lVert\int\frac{(f_{L}(\alpha)-f_{L}(\beta))(\partial_{\alpha}^{s}D_{t}\bar{\zeta}-\partial_{\beta}^{s}D_{t}\bar{\zeta})}{(\zeta(\alpha)-\zeta(\beta))^{2}}\partial_{\beta}D_{t}\zeta d\beta\right\rVert_{L^{2}}\leq

\displaystyle\ C\left\lVert\partial_{\alpha}f_{L}\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\left\lVert\partial_{\alpha}^{s}D_{t}\zeta\right\rVert_{L^{2}}\left\lVert\partial_{\alpha}D_{t}\zeta\right\rVert_{W^{1,\infty}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\ C\epsilon^{2}t^{-1-\delta}\sqrt{\mathcal{E}_{s}}.

For the high-frequency component:

\frac{2}{\pi i}\int\frac{(f_{H}(\alpha)-f_{H}(\beta))(\partial_{\alpha}^{s}D_{t}\bar{\zeta}-\partial_{\beta}^{s}D_{t}\bar{\zeta})}{(\zeta(\alpha)-\zeta(\beta))^{2}}\partial_{\beta}D_{t}\zeta d\beta,

we apply Lemma
4.15
to establish the desired decay
3
3

3

In practice, we need to raise the derivative of
f
before applying Lemma
4.15
; this process will be detailed in the treatment of
G_{12}
.
. Thus, we conclude the estimate for
\left\lVert G_{11}\right\rVert_{L^{2}}
.

Subcase:
For
G_{12}
:

We first handle the low frequency part as above. Afterward, the remaining contribution from
G_{12}
is

(5.25)

\tilde{G}_{12}=-\frac{2}{\pi i}\int\frac{(\bar{f}_{H}(\alpha)-\bar{f}_{H}(\beta))(\partial_{\alpha}^{s}D_{t}\bar{\zeta}-\partial_{\beta}^{s}D_{t}\bar{\zeta})}{(\zeta(\alpha)-\zeta(\beta))^{2}}\partial_{\beta}D_{t}\zeta d\beta.

\bullet
When
|\alpha|
is far from

.

As in the treatment of
G_{22}
, we apply Lemma
5.3
to
\tilde{G}_{12}
to get the desired decay. One should notice that

\displaystyle\left\lVert f_{H}\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\leq\left\lVert P_{>t^{2}}f\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}+\left\lVert P_{t^{-1/2-\delta}<|\cdot|\leq t^{2}}f\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}},

\displaystyle\leq Ct^{-2}\left\lVert\partial_{\alpha}P_{>t^{2}}f\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}+C\left\lVert\Lambda P_{t^{-1/2-\delta}<|\cdot|\leq t^{2}}f\right\rVert_{L^{2}}\ln t,

\displaystyle\leq C\epsilon\ln t.

\bullet
When
\alpha\in S(t)
.

Decompose
1_{S(t)}\tilde{G}_{12}
as

\displaystyle 1_{S(t)}\tilde{G}_{12}

\displaystyle=-\frac{2}{\pi i}1_{S(t)}\left(\int_{|\alpha-\beta|<t^{3/4+1/40}}+\int_{|\alpha-\beta|\geq t^{3/4+1/40}}\right)\frac{(\bar{f}_{H}(\alpha)-\bar{f}_{H}(\beta))(\partial_{\alpha}^{s}D_{t}\bar{\zeta}-\partial_{\beta}^{s}D_{t}\bar{\zeta})}{(\zeta(\alpha)-\zeta(\beta))^{2}}\partial_{\beta}D_{t}\zeta d\beta,

\displaystyle=1_{S(t)}\tilde{G}_{12}^{1}+1_{S(t)}\tilde{G}_{12}^{2}.

Subsubcase:
For
\tilde{G}_{12}^{2}
:

Using Young’s inequality, we have

\left\lVert\tilde{G}_{12}^{2}\right\rVert_{L^{2}}\leq\left\lVert\bar{f}_{H}\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\left\lVert\partial_{\alpha}^{s}D_{t}\bar{\zeta}\right\rVert_{L^{2}}\left\lVert\partial_{\alpha}D_{t}\zeta\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\int_{t^{3/4+1/40}}^{\infty}\frac{dr}{r^{2}}\leq C\epsilon^{3}t^{-\frac{5}{4}-\frac{1}{40}}\ln t.

Subsubcase:
For
\tilde{G}_{12}^{1}
, using Lemma
4.3
, write
4
4

4

Note that in the following proof we need to derive
|\alpha|\sim|\beta|
from
\alpha\in S(t)
, which implies that we must assume
3/4+1/40<1-\mu
; indeed, one may take
\mu=1/5
.

\displaystyle\tilde{G}_{12}^{1}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\pi i}\int_{|\alpha-\beta|\leq t^{3/4+1/40}}\frac{\left(\frac{2\alpha}{it}\partial_{t}\bar{f}_{H}-\frac{2\beta}{it}\partial_{t}\bar{f}_{H}\right)(\partial_{\alpha}^{s}D_{t}\bar{\zeta}-\partial_{\beta}^{s}D_{t}\bar{\zeta})}{(\zeta(\alpha)-\zeta(\beta))^{2}}\partial_{\beta}D_{t}\zeta d\beta

\displaystyle\ \ \ +\frac{1}{\pi i}\int_{|\alpha-\beta|\leq t^{3/4+1/40}}\frac{\left(\frac{2}{it}\tilde{\Omega}_{0}\bar{f}_{H}(\alpha)-\frac{2}{it}\tilde{\Omega}_{0}\bar{f}_{H}(\beta)\right)(\partial_{\alpha}^{s}D_{t}\bar{\zeta}-\partial_{\beta}^{s}D_{t}\bar{\zeta})}{(\zeta(\alpha)-\zeta(\beta))^{2}}\partial_{\beta}D_{t}\zeta d\beta.

Recall that

\left\lVert\partial_{\alpha}\tilde{\Omega}_{0}\bar{f}_{H}\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}=\left\lVert\partial_{\alpha}\Omega_{0}f_{H}\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\leq C\epsilon t^{\delta_{0}}\ln t,

Hence we can estimate the second term of
\tilde{G}_{12}^{1}
as follows:

\displaystyle\left\lVert\frac{1}{\pi i}\int_{|\alpha-\beta|\leq t^{3/4+1/40}}\frac{\left(\frac{2}{it}\tilde{\Omega}_{0}\bar{f}_{H}(\alpha)-\frac{2}{it}\tilde{\Omega}_{0}\bar{f}_{H}(\beta)\right)(\partial_{\alpha}^{s}D_{t}\bar{\zeta}-\partial_{\beta}^{s}D_{t}\bar{\zeta})}{(\zeta(\alpha)-\zeta(\beta))^{2}}\partial_{\beta}D_{t}\zeta d\beta\right\rVert_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\left\lVert\frac{2}{it}\partial_{\alpha}\tilde{\Omega}_{0}\bar{f}_{H}\right\rVert_{W^{1,\infty}}\left\lVert\partial_{\alpha}^{s}D_{t}\bar{\zeta}\right\rVert_{L^{2}}\left\lVert\partial_{\alpha}D_{t}\zeta\right\rVert_{W^{1,\infty}}\ln t,

\displaystyle\leq C\epsilon^{3}t^{-\frac{3}{2}+\delta_{0}}(\ln t)^{2}.

Decompose the first term as

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\pi i}\int_{|\alpha-\beta|\leq t^{3/4+1/40}}\frac{(\partial_{t}\bar{f}_{H}(\alpha)-\partial_{t}\bar{f}_{H}(\beta))(\partial_{\alpha}^{s}D_{t}\bar{\zeta}-\partial_{\beta}^{s}D_{t}\bar{\zeta})}{(\zeta(\alpha)-\zeta(\beta))^{2}}\frac{2\beta}{it}\partial_{\beta}D_{t}\zeta d\beta

\displaystyle\ \ \ +\frac{2}{it}\partial_{t}\bar{f}_{H}\frac{1}{\pi i}\int_{|\alpha-\beta|\leq t^{3/4+1/40}}\frac{(\alpha-\beta)(\partial_{\alpha}^{s}D_{t}\bar{\zeta}-\partial_{\beta}^{s}D_{t}\bar{\zeta})}{(\zeta(\alpha)-\zeta(\beta))^{2}}\partial_{\beta}D_{t}\zeta d\beta,

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{\pi i}\int_{|\alpha-\beta|\leq t^{3/4+1/40}}\frac{((D_{t}\bar{\zeta})_{H}(\alpha)-(D_{t}\bar{\zeta})_{H}(\beta))(\partial_{\alpha}^{s}D_{t}\bar{\zeta}-\partial_{\beta}^{s}D_{t}\bar{\zeta})}{(\zeta(\alpha)-\zeta(\beta))^{2}}(\zeta_{\beta}-1)d\beta

\displaystyle\ \ \ +\frac{2}{it}\partial_{t}\bar{f}_{H}\frac{1}{\pi i}\int_{|\alpha-\beta|\leq t^{3/4+1/40}}\frac{(\alpha-\beta)(\partial_{\alpha}^{s}D_{t}\bar{\zeta}-\partial_{\beta}^{s}D_{t}\bar{\zeta})}{(\zeta(\alpha)-\zeta(\beta))^{2}}\partial_{\beta}D_{t}\zeta d\beta+O(\epsilon^{3}t^{-\frac{5}{4}-\delta})_{L^{2}},

\displaystyle:=I+II+O(\epsilon^{3}t^{-\frac{5}{4}-\delta})_{L^{2}}.

Applying Lemma
A.3
, we obtain the following estimate for
II
:

\displaystyle\left\lVert II\right\rVert_{L^{2}}\leq\frac{C}{t}\left\lVert\partial_{t}\bar{f}_{H}\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\left\lVert\partial_{\alpha}^{s}D_{t}\bar{\zeta}\right\rVert_{L^{2}}\left\lVert\partial_{\alpha}D_{t}\zeta\right\rVert_{W^{1,\infty}}\ln t\leq C\epsilon^{3}t^{-\frac{7}{4}}\ln t.

Note that this approach is sufficient to handle the high frequency part of
G_{11}
, as it has no leading-order term according to Lemma
4.15
.
Using the Localization Lemma
4.14
(note that for
\mu\leq 1/5
, the Localization Lemma
4.14
applies to
1_{S(t)}I
), we derive

1_{S(t)}I=-1_{S(t)}\frac{it^{2}}{2\alpha^{2}}(D_{t}\bar{\zeta})_{H}\partial_{\alpha}^{s}D_{t}\bar{\zeta}(\zeta_{\alpha}-1)+O(\epsilon^{3}t^{-\frac{5}{4}-\delta})_{L^{2}},

i.e.

\displaystyle 1_{S(t)}\tilde{G}_{12}=-1_{S(t)}\frac{it^{2}}{2\alpha^{2}}(D_{t}\bar{\zeta})_{H}\partial_{\alpha}^{s}D_{t}\bar{\zeta}(\zeta_{\alpha}-1)+O(\epsilon^{3}t^{-\frac{5}{4}-\delta})_{L^{2}}.

Next, we apply the Transition of Derivatives Lemma
4.3
to rewrite the term
\frac{it^{2}}{2\alpha^{2}}(D_{t}\bar{\zeta})_{H}\partial_{\alpha}^{s}D_{t}\bar{\zeta}(\zeta_{\alpha}-1)
in the following manner:

(1)

We first distribute
\frac{it^{2}}{4\alpha^{2}}
to
\zeta_{\alpha}-1
. By Lemma
4.3
, for
\alpha\in S(t)
,

\frac{it^{2}}{4\alpha^{2}}(\zeta_{\alpha}-1)=\partial_{\alpha}(\zeta_{\alpha}-1)+O(\epsilon t^{-\frac{3}{5}+\delta_{0}}).

This yields

1_{S(t)}\tilde{G}_{12}=-1_{S(t)}2(D_{t}\bar{\zeta})_{H}\partial_{\alpha}^{s}D_{t}\bar{\zeta}\zeta_{\alpha\alpha}+(\epsilon^{3}t^{-1-\delta})_{L^{2}}.

(2)

Next, we move one derivative from
\partial_{\alpha}^{s}D_{t}\bar{\zeta}
to both
(D_{t}\bar{\zeta})_{H}
and
\zeta_{\alpha\alpha}
, respectively:

\displaystyle(D_{t}\bar{\zeta})_{H}\partial_{\alpha}^{s}D_{t}\bar{\zeta}\zeta_{\alpha\alpha}=

\displaystyle(D_{t}\bar{\zeta})_{H}\left(\frac{it^{2}}{4\alpha^{2}}\right)^{2}\partial_{\alpha}^{s-2}D_{t}\bar{\zeta}\zeta_{\alpha\alpha}+(\epsilon^{3}t^{-1-\delta})_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left(\frac{it^{2}}{4\alpha^{2}}(D_{t}\bar{\zeta})_{H}\right)\partial_{\alpha}^{s-2}D_{t}\bar{\zeta}\left(\frac{it^{2}}{4\alpha^{2}}\zeta_{\alpha\alpha}\right)+(\epsilon^{3}t^{-1-\delta})_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\partial_{\alpha}D_{t}\bar{\zeta}\partial_{\alpha}^{s-2}D_{t}\bar{\zeta}\partial_{\alpha}^{3}\zeta+(\epsilon^{3}t^{-1-\delta})_{L^{2}}.

(3)

Finally, we rewrite
\partial_{\alpha}D_{t}\bar{\zeta}
as

\partial_{\alpha}D_{t}\bar{\zeta}=\frac{\partial_{\alpha}D_{t}\bar{\zeta}}{\zeta_{\alpha}}+\partial_{\alpha}D_{t}\bar{\zeta}\left(1-\frac{1}{\zeta_{\alpha}}\right).

Substituting this expression into the formula, we obtain

\displaystyle 1_{S(t)}\tilde{G}_{12}=-2\frac{\partial_{\alpha}D_{t}\bar{\zeta}}{\zeta_{\alpha}}\partial_{\alpha}^{s-2}D_{t}\bar{\zeta}\partial_{\alpha}^{3}\zeta+(\epsilon^{3}t^{-1-\delta})_{L^{2}}.

Since
(I-\mathcal{H})\frac{\partial_{\alpha}D_{t}\bar{\zeta}}{\zeta_{\alpha}}=0
, we have

\displaystyle(I-\mathcal{H})\left(\frac{\partial_{\alpha}D_{t}\bar{\zeta}}{\zeta_{\alpha}}\partial_{\alpha}^{s-2}D_{t}\bar{\zeta}\partial_{\alpha}^{3}\zeta\right)

\displaystyle=[\partial_{\alpha}^{s-2}D_{t}\bar{\zeta}\partial_{\alpha}^{3}\zeta,\mathcal{H}]\frac{\partial_{\alpha}D_{t}\bar{\zeta}}{\zeta_{\alpha}}.

From the proof of (
4.36
), we have

\displaystyle\left\lVert\partial_{\alpha}\Big(\partial_{\alpha}^{s-2}D_{t}\bar{\zeta}\partial_{\alpha}^{3}\zeta\Big)\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\leq C\epsilon^{2}t^{-7/6+\delta_{0}}.

Hence, by Lemma
A.1
, we have

\displaystyle\left\lVert(I-\mathcal{H})\tilde{G}_{12}\right\rVert_{L^{2}}\leq

\displaystyle\ \left\lVert 2[\partial_{\alpha}^{s-2}D_{t}\bar{\zeta}\partial_{\alpha}^{3}\zeta,\mathcal{H}]\frac{\partial_{\alpha}D_{t}\bar{\zeta}}{\zeta_{\alpha}}\right\rVert_{L^{2}}+C\epsilon^{3}t^{-1-\delta^{\prime}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\ C\left\lVert\partial_{\alpha}\Big(\partial_{\alpha}^{s-2}D_{t}\bar{\zeta}\partial_{\alpha}^{3}\zeta\Big)\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\left\lVert D_{t}\bar{\zeta}\right\rVert_{L^{2}}+C\epsilon^{3}t^{-1-\delta^{\prime}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\ C\epsilon^{3}t^{-1-\delta^{\prime}}.

This completes the proof.

∎

5.3.
Estimate
E_{s}^{\sigma}

Recall that

\frac{dE_{s}^{\sigma}}{dt}=Re\left\{\int\frac{2D_{t}\bar{\sigma}_{s}}{A}G_{s}^{\sigma}d\alpha\right\}-\int\frac{|D_{t}\sigma_{s}|^{2}}{A}\left(\frac{a_{t}}{a}\circ\kappa^{-1}\right)d\alpha,

where

G_{s}^{\sigma}=\partial_{\alpha}^{s}D_{t}G+i\partial_{\alpha}^{s}\left(\frac{a_{t}}{a}\circ\kappa^{-1}A\theta_{\alpha}\right)+[D_{t}^{2}-iA\partial_{\alpha},\partial_{\alpha}^{s}]\sigma,

As for
G_{s}^{\theta}
, we have the following estimate.

Lemma 5.5
.

There holds

\left|\int\frac{D_{t}\bar{\sigma}_{k}}{A}[D_{t}^{2}-iA\partial_{\alpha},\partial_{\alpha}^{k}]\sigma d\alpha\right|\leq C\epsilon^{4}t^{-1-\delta},\ \forall 1\leq k\leq s.

Proof.

Repeat the proof for Lemma
5.4
yields the result.
∎

Also, we need to estimate the extra term that doesn’t appear in
G_{\theta}^{s}
.

Lemma 5.6
.

There exists
\delta>0
such that

\left\lVert\left(\frac{a_{t}}{a}\circ\kappa^{-1}\right)A\theta_{\alpha}\right\rVert_{H^{s}}\leq C\epsilon^{3}t^{-1-\delta}.

Proof.

We only verify the
L^{2}
norm of
s
-order derivatives.
Actually, we have (according to Proposition
4.3
)

\left\lVert\partial_{\alpha}^{s}\left(\frac{a_{t}}{a}\circ\kappa^{-1}\right)\cdot A\theta_{\alpha}\right\rVert_{L^{2}}\leq C\left\lVert\frac{a_{t}}{a}\circ\kappa^{-1}\right\rVert_{H^{s}}\left\lVert\theta_{\alpha}\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\leq C\epsilon^{3}t^{-1-\delta}.

A similar estimate holds when the derivatives apply to
A
. In addition, there holds

\left\lVert\left(\frac{a_{t}}{a}\circ\kappa^{-1}\right)A\partial_{\alpha}^{s}\theta_{\alpha}\right\rVert_{L^{2}}\leq C\left\lVert\frac{a_{t}}{a}\circ\kappa^{-1}\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\left\lVert\theta_{\alpha}\right\rVert_{H^{s}}\leq C\epsilon^{3}t^{-1-\delta}.

This implies the conclusion.
∎

Proposition 5.2
.

There holds

(5.26)

\left|\frac{d}{dt}E_{s}^{\sigma}(t)\right|\leq C\epsilon^{2}t^{-1-\delta^{\prime}}\mathcal{E}_{s}+C\epsilon^{4}t^{-1-\delta^{\prime}}

for some
\delta^{\prime}>0
.

Proof.

It suffices to consider

Re\left\{\int\frac{2D_{t}\bar{\sigma}_{s}}{A}\partial_{\alpha}^{s}D_{t}Gd\alpha\right\}.

Furthermore, by Lemma
4.9
, we have

\int\frac{D_{t}\bar{\sigma}_{s}}{A}\partial_{\alpha}^{s}D_{t}Gd\alpha=2\int\frac{\partial_{\alpha}^{s}D_{t}^{2}\bar{\zeta}}{A}\partial_{\alpha}^{s}D_{t}Gd\alpha+O(\epsilon^{4}t^{-1-\delta}),

where the estimate
\left\lVert\partial_{\alpha}^{s}D_{t}G\right\rVert_{L^{2}}\leq C\epsilon^{3}t^{-1}\ln t
follows from Lemma
A.3
. Next, leveraging the almost-holomorphic nature of
\partial_{\alpha}^{s}D_{t}^{2}\bar{\zeta}
, we deduce

2\int\frac{\partial_{\alpha}^{s}D_{t}^{2}\bar{\zeta}}{A}(I-\mathcal{H})\partial_{\alpha}^{s}D_{t}Gd\alpha=4\int\frac{\partial_{\alpha}^{s}D_{t}^{2}\bar{\zeta}}{A}\partial_{\alpha}^{s}D_{t}Gd\alpha+O(\epsilon^{4}t^{-1-\delta^{\prime}}).

This reduces the problem to controlling
\partial_{\alpha}^{s}D_{t}G
or
(I-\mathcal{H})\partial_{\alpha}^{s}D_{t}G
, depending on our preference. Since these results will be essential in Chapter 7, we detail the treatment of some specific components of
D_{t}G
which closely resemble the approach in Proposition
5.1
. We first consider the derivatives of
G_{2}
. Observe that

\displaystyle\partial_{t}\frac{1}{\pi i}\int\left(\frac{z_{t}(\alpha)-z_{t}(\beta)}{z(\alpha)-z(\beta)}\right)^{2}(z-\bar{z})_{\beta}d\beta

\displaystyle=\frac{2}{\pi i}\int\frac{z_{t}(\alpha)-z_{t}(\beta)}{z(\alpha)-z(\beta)}\partial_{t}\left(\frac{z_{t}(\alpha)-z_{t}(\beta)}{z(\alpha)-z(\beta)}\right)(z-\bar{z})_{\beta}d\beta

\displaystyle\ \ \ +\frac{1}{\pi i}\int\left(\frac{z_{t}(\alpha)-z_{t}(\beta)}{z(\alpha)-z(\beta)}\right)^{2}(z_{t}-\bar{z}_{t})_{\beta}d\beta,

\displaystyle=-\frac{2}{\pi i}\int\frac{z_{t}(\alpha)-z_{t}(\beta)}{z(\alpha)-z(\beta)}\left(\frac{z_{t}(\alpha)-z_{t}(\beta)}{z(\alpha)-z(\beta)}\right)^{2}(z-\bar{z})_{\beta}d\beta

\displaystyle\ \ \ +\frac{2}{\pi i}\int\frac{(z_{t}(\alpha)-z_{t}(\beta))(z_{tt}(\alpha)-z_{tt}(\beta))}{(z(\alpha)-z(\beta))^{2}}(z-\bar{z})_{\beta}d\beta

\displaystyle\ \ \ +\frac{1}{\pi i}\int\left(\frac{z_{t}(\alpha)-z_{t}(\beta)}{z(\alpha)-z(\beta)}\right)^{2}(z_{t}-\bar{z}_{t})_{\beta}d\beta.

It is clear that the first term is easier to estimate than the others since it is quartic, whereas the remaining terms are cubic. Therefore, for
\partial_{\alpha}^{s}D_{t}G_{2}
we focus on analyzing the following expression:

\frac{2}{\pi i}\partial_{\alpha}^{s}\int\frac{(z_{t}(\alpha)-z_{t}(\beta))(z_{tt}(\alpha)-z_{tt}(\beta))}{(z(\alpha)-z(\beta))^{2}}(z-\bar{z})_{\beta}d\beta+\frac{1}{\pi i}\partial_{\alpha}^{s}\int\left(\frac{z_{t}(\alpha)-z_{t}(\beta)}{z(\alpha)-z(\beta)}\right)^{2}(z_{t}-\bar{z}_{t})_{\beta}d\beta.

Like we did in Proposition
5.1
, we only provide the estimate for the extreme case:

(5.27)

\displaystyle I

\displaystyle=\frac{2}{\pi i}\int\frac{(D_{t}\zeta(\alpha)-D_{t}\zeta(\beta))(\partial_{\alpha}^{s}D_{t}^{2}\zeta-\partial_{\beta}^{s}D_{t}^{2}\zeta)}{(\zeta(\alpha)-\zeta(\beta))^{2}}(\zeta-\bar{\zeta})_{\beta}d\beta

\displaystyle\overset{\text{def}}{=}I_{1}+I_{2},

where

I_{1}=\frac{2}{\pi i}\int\frac{(D_{t}\zeta(\alpha)-D_{t}\zeta(\beta))(\partial_{\alpha}^{s}D_{t}^{2}\zeta-\partial_{\beta}D_{t}^{2}\zeta)}{(\zeta(\alpha)-\zeta(\beta))^{2}}(\zeta_{\beta}-1)d\beta,

I_{2}=\frac{2}{\pi i}\int\frac{(D_{t}\zeta(\alpha)-D_{t}\zeta(\beta))(\partial_{\alpha}D_{t}^{2}\zeta-\partial_{\beta}D_{t}^{2}\zeta)}{(\zeta(\alpha)-\zeta(\beta))^{2}}(\bar{\zeta}_{\beta}-1)d\beta.

We claim that

(5.28)

1_{S(t)}I=-\frac{it}{\alpha}\partial_{\alpha}^{s}D_{t}^{2}\zeta|\zeta_{\alpha}-1|^{2}+O(\epsilon^{3}t^{-\frac{5}{4}-\delta})_{L^{2}}.

First, it is straightforward to verify that the arguments used for
G_{21}
apply similarly to
I_{1}
. Thus, we obtain

\left\lVert I_{1}\right\rVert_{L^{2}}\leq C\epsilon^{3}t^{-\frac{3}{2}}\ln t.

For
1_{S(t)}I_{2}
, we use frequency decomposition
f_{L}=P_{<t^{-2}}f,\ f=f-f_{L}
, and apply the Localization Lemma, together with equation
(D_{t}^{2}-iA\partial_{\alpha})\zeta=-i
to obtain

\displaystyle 1_{S(t)}I_{2}

\displaystyle=1_{S(t)}\frac{it^{2}}{2\alpha^{2}}(D_{t}\zeta)_{H}\partial_{\alpha}^{s}D_{t}^{2}\zeta(\bar{\zeta}_{\alpha}-1)+O(\epsilon^{3}t^{-\frac{5}{4}-\delta})_{L^{2}},

\displaystyle=-1_{S(t)}\frac{t}{\alpha}(D_{t}^{2}\zeta)\partial_{\alpha}^{s}D_{t}^{2}\zeta(\bar{\zeta}_{\alpha}-1)+O(\epsilon^{3}t^{-\frac{5}{4}-\delta})_{L^{2}},

\displaystyle=-\frac{it}{\alpha}\partial_{\alpha}^{s}D_{t}^{2}\zeta|\zeta_{\alpha}-1|^{2}+O(\epsilon^{3}t^{-\frac{5}{4}-\delta})_{L^{2}}.

Hence, (
5.28
) is established. The term
1_{S(t)^{c}}I
is handled using Lemma
5.3
, as in Proposition
5.1
.
Finally, we obtain

\displaystyle 2Re\int\frac{D_{t}\bar{\sigma}_{s}}{A}Id\alpha

\displaystyle=4Re\int\frac{\partial_{\alpha}^{s}D_{t}^{2}\bar{\zeta}}{A}1_{S(t)}Id\alpha+O(\epsilon^{4}t^{-1-\delta})

\displaystyle=-4Re\int\frac{it}{A\alpha}|\partial_{\alpha}^{s}D_{t}^{2}\zeta|^{2}|\zeta_{\alpha}-1|^{2}d\alpha+O(\epsilon^{4}t^{-1-\delta})

\displaystyle=O(\epsilon^{4}t^{-1-\delta}).

as desired.

Next, we consider the corresponding term of
\tilde{G}_{12}
in
D_{t}G
(recall that
f_{H}=P_{>t^{-1/2-\delta}}f
and
f_{L}=f-f_{H}
here):

\tilde{G}_{12}^{*}=\frac{1}{\pi i}\int\frac{(\bar{f}_{H}(\alpha)-\bar{f}_{H}(\beta))(\partial_{\alpha}^{s}D_{t}^{2}\bar{\zeta}-\partial_{\beta}^{s}D_{t}^{2}\bar{\zeta})}{(\zeta(\alpha)-\zeta(\beta))^{2}}\partial_{\beta}D_{t}\zeta d\beta.

Actually, it can be treated using similar arguments as those in the proof of Proposition
5.1
, since we always estimate
\partial_{\alpha}^{s}D_{t}\zeta
in
L^{2}
, and there is no difficulty in replacing
\partial_{\alpha}^{s}D_{t}\zeta
by
\partial_{\alpha}^{s}D_{t}^{2}\zeta
. From now on, if a proof of an estimate is omitted, we mean that it can be proved by simply repeating the relevant part in Proposition
5.1
.

We only consider the case
\alpha\in S(t)
. Decompose the integral domain into
|\alpha-\beta|\leq t^{3/4+1/40}
and
|\alpha-\beta|>t^{3/4+1/40}
. For the second part, we have the estimate

\displaystyle\left\lVert\frac{1}{\pi i}\int_{|\alpha-\beta|>t^{3/4+1/40}}\frac{(\bar{f}_{H}(\alpha)-\bar{f}_{H}(\beta))(\partial_{\alpha}^{s}D_{t}^{2}\bar{\zeta}-\partial_{\beta}^{s}D_{t}^{2}\bar{\zeta})}{(\zeta(\alpha)-\zeta(\beta))^{2}}\partial_{\beta}D_{t}\zeta d\beta\right\rVert_{L^{2}}\leq C\epsilon^{3}t^{-\frac{5}{4}-\frac{1}{40}}\ln t.

Next, we decompose the first part in the same manner as before:

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\pi i}\int_{|\alpha-\beta|\leq t^{3/4+1/40}}\frac{\left(\frac{2\alpha}{it}\partial_{t}\bar{f}_{H}-\frac{2\beta}{it}\partial_{t}\bar{f}_{H}\right)(\partial_{\alpha}^{s}D_{t}^{2}\bar{\zeta}-\partial_{\beta}^{s}D_{t}^{2}\bar{\zeta})}{(\zeta(\alpha)-\zeta(\beta))^{2}}\partial_{\beta}D_{t}\zeta d\beta

\displaystyle\ \ \ +\frac{1}{\pi i}\int_{|\alpha-\beta|\leq t^{3/4+1/40}}\frac{\left(\frac{2}{it}\tilde{\Omega}_{0}\bar{f}_{H}(\alpha)-\frac{2}{it}\tilde{\Omega}_{0}\bar{f}_{H}(\beta)\right)(\partial_{\alpha}^{s}D_{t}^{2}\bar{\zeta}-\partial_{\beta}^{s}D_{t}^{2}\bar{\zeta})}{(\zeta(\alpha)-\zeta(\beta))^{2}}\partial_{\beta}D_{t}\zeta d\beta

For the second term, we obtain the following bound:

\displaystyle\left\lVert\frac{1}{\pi i}\int_{|\alpha-\beta|\leq t^{3/4+1/40}}\frac{\left(\frac{2}{it}\tilde{\Omega}_{0}\bar{f}_{H}(\alpha)-\frac{2}{it}\tilde{\Omega}_{0}\bar{f}_{H}(\beta)\right)(\partial_{\alpha}^{s}D_{t}^{2}\bar{\zeta}-\partial_{\beta}^{s}D_{t}^{2}\bar{\zeta})}{(\zeta(\alpha)-\zeta(\beta))^{2}}\partial_{\beta}D_{t}\zeta d\beta\right\rVert_{L^{2}}\leq C\epsilon^{3}t^{-\frac{3}{2}+\delta_{0}}(\ln t)^{2}.

For the first term, we have

\displaystyle 1_{S(t)}\frac{1}{\pi i}\int_{|\alpha-\beta|\leq t^{3/4+1/40}}\frac{\left(\frac{2\alpha}{it}\partial_{t}\bar{f}_{H}-\frac{2\beta}{it}\partial_{t}\bar{f}_{H}\right)(\partial_{\alpha}^{s}D_{t}^{2}\bar{\zeta}-\partial_{\beta}^{s}D_{t}^{2}\bar{\zeta})}{(\zeta(\alpha)-\zeta(\beta))^{2}}\partial_{\beta}D_{t}\zeta d\beta

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\ -1_{S(t)}\frac{it^{2}}{2\alpha^{2}}(D_{t}\bar{\zeta})_{H}\partial_{\alpha}^{s}D_{t}^{2}\bar{\zeta}(\zeta_{\alpha}-1)+O(\epsilon^{3}t^{-\frac{5}{4}-\delta})_{L^{2}}.

Now all we need is to transit the derivative and make an estimate similar to the parallel argument in Proposition
5.1
and obtain

(5.29)

(I-\mathcal{H})\tilde{G}_{12}^{*}=O(\epsilon^{3}t^{-\frac{5}{4}-\delta})_{L^{2}}

for some
\delta>0
. This completes the proof.
∎

5.4.
A priori control of the energy estimates

In conclusion, we get the energy estimate for
\mathcal{E}_{s}
by summing up all of the estimates above and conclude the proof of Theorem
5.1
. As a corollary, we have

Corollary 5.1
.

There holds

(5.30)

\mathcal{E}_{s}(t)\leq C\epsilon^{2}\left(e^{\frac{C\epsilon^{2}}{\delta}-\frac{C\epsilon^{2}}{\delta}(1+t)^{-\delta}}-1\right)+e^{\frac{C\epsilon^{2}}{\delta}-\frac{C\epsilon^{2}}{\delta}(1+t)^{-\delta}}\mathcal{E}_{s}(0).

Proof.

We may rewrite (
5.14
) as

\left|\frac{d}{dt}\mathcal{E}_{s}(t)\right|\leq C\epsilon^{2}(1+t)^{-1-\delta}\mathcal{E}_{s}(t)+O(\epsilon^{4}(1+t)^{-1-\delta})

since each relevant term is bounded. Now we have

\frac{d}{dt}e^{\frac{C\epsilon^{2}}{\delta}(1+t)^{-\delta}}\mathcal{E}_{s}(t)\leq C^{\prime}\epsilon^{4}(1+t)^{-1-\delta}e^{\frac{C\epsilon^{2}}{\delta}(1+t)^{-\delta}}

which implies

\mathcal{E}_{s}(t)\leq\frac{C^{\prime}\epsilon^{2}}{C}\left(e^{\frac{C\epsilon^{2}}{\delta}-\frac{C\epsilon^{2}}{\delta}(1+t)^{-\delta}}-1\right)+e^{\frac{C\epsilon^{2}}{\delta}-\frac{C\epsilon^{2}}{\delta}(1+t)^{-\delta}}\mathcal{E}_{s}(0).

∎

Corollary 5.2

(Almost conservation law)
.

We have for any
t\in[0,T]
,

(5.31)

|\mathcal{E}_{s}(t)-\mathcal{E}_{s}(0)|\leq C\epsilon^{4}.

Proof.

Combining Theorem
5.1
and Corollary
5.1
,

\displaystyle\frac{d\mathcal{E}_{s}(t)}{dt}\leq C\epsilon^{2}(1+t)^{-1-\delta}\mathcal{E}_{s}(t)+C\epsilon^{4}(1+t)^{-1-\delta}\leq C\epsilon^{4}(1+t)^{-1-\delta}.

Therefore,

\displaystyle\left|\mathcal{E}_{s}(t)-\mathcal{E}_{s}(0)\right|\leq C\int_{0}^{t}\epsilon^{4}(1+\tau)^{-1-\delta}d\tau\leq C\epsilon^{4}.

This establishes (
5.31
).
∎

6.
Vector field estimates

In this section, we improve the vector field estimate in the bootstrap assumption. Firstly, we estimate
[L_{0},D_{t}^{2}-iA\partial_{\alpha}]\partial_{\alpha}^{s-1}f
where
f=\theta
or
D_{t}\theta
. Keeping in mind that we can control at most
s-1
derivatives of
L_{0}D_{t}\zeta
and
s-1
derivatives of
L_{0}D_{t}^{2}\zeta
(or other equivalent quantities).

Lemma 6.1
.

Suppose that
f=\theta
or
D_{t}\theta
.
Then there holds

\displaystyle\left\lVert[L_{0},D_{t}^{2}-iA\partial_{\alpha}]\partial_{\alpha}^{s-1}f\right\rVert_{L^{2}(|\alpha|\geq t)}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\ C\epsilon^{2}t^{-1}(\left\lVert L_{0}(\zeta_{\alpha}-1)\right\rVert_{L^{2}}+\left\lVert L_{0}D_{t}\zeta\right\rVert_{H^{1}}+\left\lVert L_{0}(\zeta-\alpha)\right\rVert_{\dot{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}})+C\epsilon^{3}t^{-1}.

Proof.

Recall that

(6.1)

[L_{0},D_{t}^{2}-iA\partial_{\alpha}]=-(D_{t}^{2}-iA\partial_{\alpha})+\Big\{L_{0}D_{t}b-(L_{0}b-\frac{1}{2}b)b_{\alpha}\Big\}\partial_{\alpha}+\Big(L_{0}b-\frac{1}{2}b\Big)(D_{t}\partial_{\alpha}+\partial_{\alpha}D_{t})-i(L_{0}A)\partial_{\alpha}.

We point out that the most difficult part is to estimate
\|L_{0}b\cdot D_{t}\partial_{\alpha}\partial_{\alpha}^{s-1}f\|_{L^{2}}
, since there are
s
derivatives on
D_{t}f
while there are no derivatives on
b
.

We first study
L_{0}b
since we only have the formula of
(I-\mathcal{H})b
. Actually, according to Lemma
4.7
(note that
L_{0}b
is real), we have

\left\lVert L_{0}b\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\leq C\left\lVert(I-\mathcal{H})L_{0}b\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\leq C\left\lVert L_{0}(I-\mathcal{H})b\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}+C\left\lVert[L_{0},\mathcal{H}]b\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}.

Note that

[L_{0},\mathcal{H}]b=[L_{0}\zeta,\mathcal{H}]\frac{\partial_{\alpha}b}{\zeta_{\alpha}}

which implies

\left\lVert[L_{0},\mathcal{H}]b\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\leq C\left\lVert L_{0}(\zeta_{\alpha}-1)\right\rVert_{W^{1,\infty}}\left\lVert\partial_{\alpha}b\right\rVert_{W^{1,\infty}}\ln t\leq C\epsilon^{3}t^{-\frac{3}{2}+\delta+\delta_{0}}

for any
\delta>0
. Here we have used Lemma
A.1
and Lemma
4.13
. This gives

\displaystyle\left\lVert L_{0}b\cdot D_{t}\partial_{\alpha}^{s}f\right\rVert_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq C\left\lVert L_{0}(I-\mathcal{H})b\cdot D_{t}\partial_{\alpha}^{s}f\right\rVert_{L^{2}}+C\left\lVert[L_{0},\mathcal{H}]b\cdot D_{t}\partial_{\alpha}^{s}f\right\rVert_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq C\left\lVert L_{0}(I-\mathcal{H})b\cdot D_{t}\partial_{\alpha}^{s}f\right\rVert_{L^{2}}+C\epsilon^{4}t^{-\frac{3}{2}+\delta+\delta_{0}},\ \forall\delta>0.

Hence it suffices to consider
\left\lVert L_{0}(I-\mathcal{H})bD_{t}\partial_{\alpha}^{s}f\right\rVert_{L^{2}}
in the following proof. Define

(I-\mathcal{H})b=\frac{1}{\pi i}\int\frac{D_{t}\zeta(\alpha)-D_{t}\zeta(\beta)}{\zeta(\alpha)-\zeta(\beta)}(\bar{\zeta}_{\beta}-1)d\beta\overset{\text{def}}{=}\int K(\alpha,\beta,t)(\bar{\zeta}_{\beta}-1)d\beta\overset{\text{def}}{=}K(\bar{\zeta}_{\alpha}-1)

Note that

\displaystyle L_{0}(I-\mathcal{H})b

\displaystyle=KL_{0}(\bar{\zeta}_{\alpha}-1)+[L_{0},K](\bar{\zeta}_{\alpha}-1),

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\pi i}\int\frac{D_{t}\zeta(\alpha)-D_{t}\zeta(\beta)}{\zeta(\alpha)-\zeta(\beta)}L_{0}(\bar{\zeta}_{\beta}-1)d\beta+\frac{1}{\pi i}\int\left(\alpha\partial_{\alpha}+\beta\partial_{\beta}+\frac{1}{2}t\partial_{t}\right)K(\alpha,\beta,t)(\bar{\zeta}_{\beta}-1)d\beta

\displaystyle+K(\bar{\zeta}_{\alpha}-1),

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\pi i}\int\frac{D_{t}\zeta(\alpha)-D_{t}\zeta(\beta)}{\zeta(\alpha)-\zeta(\beta)}L_{0}(\bar{\zeta}_{\beta}-1)d\beta+\frac{1}{\pi i}\int\frac{L_{0}D_{t}\zeta(\alpha)-L_{0}D_{t}\zeta(\beta)}{\zeta(\alpha)-\zeta(\beta)}(\bar{\zeta}_{\beta}-1)d\beta

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{\pi i}\int\frac{(L_{0}\zeta(\alpha)-L_{0}\zeta(\beta))(D_{t}\zeta(\alpha)-D_{t}\zeta(\beta))}{(\zeta(\alpha)-\zeta(\beta))^{2}}(\bar{\zeta}_{\beta}-1)d\beta+(I-\mathcal{H})b,

\displaystyle=I+II+III+(I-\mathcal{H})b.

We begin with the term
ID_{t}\partial_{\alpha}^{s}f
and rewrite it as

(6.2)

ID_{t}\partial_{\alpha}^{s}f=D_{t}\partial_{\alpha}^{s}f(\alpha,t)\Big(\int_{|\alpha-\beta|\leq t/2}+\int_{|\alpha-\beta|\geq t/2}\Big)\frac{D_{t}\zeta(\alpha)-D_{t}\zeta(\beta)}{\zeta(\alpha)-\zeta(\beta)}L_{0}(\bar{\zeta}_{\beta}-1)d\beta.

We have

\displaystyle D_{t}\partial_{\alpha}^{s}f(\alpha,t)\int_{|\alpha-\beta|\geq t/2}\frac{D_{t}\zeta(\alpha)-D_{t}\zeta(\beta)}{\zeta(\alpha)-\zeta(\beta)}L_{0}(\bar{\zeta}_{\beta}-1)d\beta

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\pi i}D_{t}\partial_{\alpha}^{s}fD_{t}\zeta(\alpha,t)\int_{|\alpha-\beta|\geq t/2}\frac{L_{0}(\bar{\zeta}_{\beta}-1)}{\zeta(\alpha)-\zeta(\beta)}d\beta-\frac{1}{\pi i}D_{t}\partial_{\alpha}^{s}f(\alpha,t)\int_{|\alpha-\beta|\geq t/2}\frac{D_{t}\zeta(\beta)}{\zeta(\alpha)-\zeta(\beta)}L_{0}(\bar{\zeta}_{\beta}-1)d\beta

For the first term, by Lemma
4.12
(
f=\theta
or
D_{t}\theta
), the
L^{2}
boundedness of the truncated singular integrals, we have

\displaystyle\left\lVert D_{t}\partial_{\alpha}^{s}fD_{t}\zeta(\alpha,t)\int_{|\alpha-\beta|\geq t/2}\frac{L_{0}(\bar{\zeta}_{\beta}-1)}{\zeta(\alpha)-\zeta(\beta)}d\beta\right\rVert_{L^{2}(|\alpha|\geq t)}\leq

\displaystyle\ \left\lVert D_{t}\partial_{\alpha}^{s}fD_{t}\zeta\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(|\alpha|\geq t)}\left\lVert\int_{|\alpha-\beta|\geq t/2}\frac{L_{0}(\bar{\zeta}_{\beta}-1)}{\zeta(\alpha)-\zeta(\beta)}d\beta\right\rVert_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\ C\epsilon^{2}t^{-1}\left\lVert L_{0}(\zeta_{\alpha}-1)\right\rVert_{L^{2}}.

For the second term, we have

\displaystyle\left\lVert D_{t}\partial_{\alpha}^{s}f(\alpha,t)\int_{|\alpha-\beta|\geq t/2}\frac{D_{t}\zeta(\beta)}{\zeta(\alpha)-\zeta(\beta)}L_{0}(\bar{\zeta}_{\beta}-1)d\beta\right\rVert_{L^{2}(|\alpha|\geq t)}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\ \left\lVert D_{t}\partial_{\alpha}^{s}f\right\rVert_{L^{2}}\left\lVert\int_{|\alpha-\beta|\geq t/2}\frac{D_{t}\zeta(\beta)}{\zeta(\alpha)-\zeta(\beta)}L_{0}(\bar{\zeta}_{\beta}-1)d\beta\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\ Ct^{-1}\left\lVert D_{t}\partial_{\alpha}^{s}f\right\rVert_{L^{2}}\left\lVert D_{t}\zeta\right\rVert_{L^{2}}\left\lVert L_{0}(\zeta_{\alpha}-1)\right\rVert_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\ C\epsilon^{2}t^{-1}\left\lVert L_{0}(\zeta_{\alpha}-1)\right\rVert_{L^{2}}.

For
|\alpha|\geq t
and
|\alpha-\beta|\leq t/2
, we have
\frac{1}{2}|\alpha|\leq|\beta|\leq 2|\alpha|
. Apply Lemma
4.12
and we have

\displaystyle\left\lVert D_{t}\partial_{\alpha}^{s}f(\alpha,t)\int_{|\alpha-\beta|\leq t/2}\frac{D_{t}\zeta(\alpha)-D_{t}\zeta(\beta)}{\zeta(\alpha)-\zeta(\beta)}L_{0}(\bar{\zeta}_{\beta}-1)d\beta\right\rVert_{L^{2}(|\alpha|\geq t)}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\ \left\lVert D_{t}\partial_{\alpha}^{s}fD_{t}\zeta(\alpha)\int_{|\alpha-\beta|\leq t/2}\frac{L_{0}(\bar{\zeta}_{\beta}-1)}{\zeta(\alpha)-\zeta(\beta)}d\beta\right\rVert_{L^{2}(|\alpha|\geq t)}+\left\lVert\int_{|\alpha-\beta|\leq t/2}\frac{D_{t}\partial_{\alpha}^{s}f(\alpha,t)D_{t}\zeta(\beta)}{\zeta(\alpha)-\zeta(\beta)}L_{0}(\bar{\zeta}_{\beta}-1)d\beta\right\rVert_{L^{2}(|\alpha|\geq t)}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\ \left\lVert D_{t}\partial_{\alpha}^{s}fD_{t}\zeta\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(|\alpha|\geq t)}\left\lVert L_{0}(\zeta_{\alpha}-1)\right\rVert_{L^{2}}+\sup_{\frac{|\alpha|}{2}\leq|\beta|\leq 2|\alpha|}|D_{t}\zeta(\beta,t)D_{t}\partial_{\alpha}^{s}f|\left\lVert L_{0}(\zeta_{\alpha}-1)\right\rVert_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\ C\epsilon^{2}t^{-1}\left\lVert L_{0}(\zeta_{\alpha}-1)\right\rVert_{L^{2}}.

Next we consider II, i.e.

\left\|\frac{1}{\pi i}D_{t}\partial_{\alpha}^{s}f\int\frac{L_{0}D_{t}\zeta(\alpha)-L_{0}D_{t}\zeta(\beta)}{\zeta(\alpha)-\zeta(\beta)}(\bar{\zeta}_{\beta}-1)d\beta\right\|_{L^{2}(|\alpha|\geq t)}.

At first, note that

\left\lVert\frac{1}{\pi i}D_{t}\partial_{\alpha}^{s}fL_{0}D_{t}\zeta(\alpha)\int\frac{\bar{\zeta}_{\beta}-1}{\zeta(\alpha)-\zeta(\beta)}d\beta\right\rVert_{L^{2}(|\alpha|\geq t)}=\left\lVert D_{t}\partial_{\alpha}^{s}f\frac{\bar{\zeta}_{\alpha}-1}{\zeta_{\alpha}}L_{0}D_{t}\zeta\right\rVert_{L^{2}(|\alpha|\geq t)}\leq C\epsilon^{2}t^{-1}\left\lVert L_{0}D_{t}\zeta\right\rVert_{L^{2}}

according to Lemma
4.12
. Then, since
|\alpha-\beta|\leq t/2
implies
|\alpha|/2\leq|\beta|\leq 2|\alpha|
, there holds

\displaystyle\left\|D_{t}\partial_{\alpha}^{s}f\int_{|\alpha-\beta|\leq t/2}\frac{L_{0}D_{t}\zeta(\beta)}{\zeta(\alpha)-\zeta(\beta)}(\bar{\zeta}_{\beta}-1)d\beta\right\|_{L^{2}(|\alpha|\geq t)}

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{\frac{|\alpha|}{2}\leq|\beta|\leq 2|\alpha|}|(\bar{\zeta}_{\beta}-1)D_{t}\partial_{\alpha}^{s}f|\left\lVert L_{0}D_{t}\zeta\right\rVert_{L^{2}},

\displaystyle\leq C\epsilon^{2}t^{-1}\left\lVert L_{0}D_{t}\zeta\right\rVert_{L^{2}}.

Finally,

\displaystyle\left\|D_{t}\partial_{\alpha}^{s}f\int_{|\alpha-\beta|\geq t/2}\frac{L_{0}D_{t}\zeta(\beta)}{\zeta(\alpha)-\zeta(\beta)}(\bar{\zeta}_{\beta}-1)d\beta\right\|_{L^{2}(|\alpha|\geq t)}

\displaystyle\leq C\epsilon\int_{|\alpha-\beta|\geq t/2}\left|\frac{L_{0}D_{t}\zeta(\beta)}{\zeta(\alpha)-\zeta(\beta)}(\bar{\zeta}_{\beta}-1)\right|d\beta,

\displaystyle\leq C\epsilon t^{-1}\left\lVert\bar{\zeta}_{\alpha}-1\right\rVert_{L^{2}}\left\lVert L_{0}D_{t}\zeta\right\rVert_{L^{2}},

\displaystyle\leq C\epsilon^{2}t^{-1}\left\lVert L_{0}D_{t}\zeta\right\rVert_{L^{2}}.

The proof for III is easier than I,II and we shall omit it. Also, it’s obvious that the proof for I,II works for
(I-\mathcal{H})b
and gives

\left\lVert D_{t}\partial_{\alpha}^{s}f(I-\mathcal{H})b\right\rVert_{L^{2}(|\alpha|\geq t)}\leq C\epsilon^{3}t^{-1}.

This completes the proof.
∎

Remark 6.1
.

There is no substantive difficulty in extending this proof to
\left\lVert[L_{0},D_{t}^{2}-iA\partial_{\alpha}]\partial_{\alpha}^{k}f\right\rVert_{L^{2}(|\alpha|\geq t)}
for
0\leq k\leq s-2
, since the expression of the commutator indicates that there is at least 1 derivative on
f
(except for the term
(D_{t}^{2}-iA\partial_{\alpha})f
; however, such a term is cubic and therefore decays in
L^{2}
at a rate
t^{-1}
).

Proposition 6.1
.

Under the assumption of Lemma
6.1
, there holds

(6.3)

\displaystyle\left\lVert[L_{0},D_{t}^{2}-iA\partial_{\alpha}]\partial_{\alpha}^{s-1}f\right\rVert_{L^{2}(R)}

\displaystyle\leq C\epsilon^{2}t^{-1+\delta_{0}}\ln t(\left\lVert L_{0}(\bar{\zeta}_{\alpha}-1)\right\rVert_{L^{2}}+\left\lVert L_{0}(\zeta-\alpha)\right\rVert_{\dot{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}}+\left\lVert L_{0}D_{t}\zeta\right\rVert_{L^{2}})

\displaystyle+C\epsilon^{3}t^{-1}.

Proof.

It’s enough for us to estimate
\left\lVert L_{0}(I-\mathcal{H})bD_{t}\partial_{\alpha}^{s}f\right\rVert_{L^{2}(|\alpha|\leq t)}
. Define
f_{L}=P_{<t^{-1}}f
,
f_{H}=f-f_{L}
.

We first consider I. Note that

\left\lVert(D_{t}\zeta)_{L}\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\leq t^{-\frac{1}{2}}\left\lVert(D_{t}\zeta)_{L}\right\rVert_{L^{2}}\leq C\epsilon t^{-\frac{1}{2}},

hence

\displaystyle\left\lVert\int_{|\alpha-\beta|\geq t}\frac{(D_{t}\zeta)_{L}(\alpha)-(D_{t}\zeta)_{L}(\beta)}{\zeta(\alpha)-\zeta(\beta)}L_{0}(\bar{\zeta}_{\beta}-1)d\beta\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\leq Ct^{-\frac{1}{2}}\left\lVert(D_{t}\zeta)_{L}\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\left\lVert L_{0}(\bar{\zeta}_{\alpha}-1)\right\rVert_{L^{2}},

\displaystyle\leq C\epsilon t^{-1}\left\lVert L_{0}(\bar{\zeta}_{\alpha}-1)\right\rVert_{L^{2}}.

Also,

\displaystyle\left\lVert\int_{|\alpha-\beta|\leq t}\frac{(D_{t}\zeta)_{L}(\alpha)-(D_{t}\zeta)_{L}(\beta)}{\zeta(\alpha)-\zeta(\beta)}L_{0}(\bar{\zeta}_{\beta}-1)d\beta\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\leq C\left\lVert\partial_{\alpha}(D_{t}\zeta)_{L}\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}t^{\frac{1}{2}}\left\lVert L_{0}(\bar{\zeta}_{\alpha}-1)\right\rVert_{L^{2}}.

While

\left\lVert\partial_{\alpha}(D_{t}\zeta)_{L}\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\leq t^{-1}\left\lVert(D_{t}\zeta)_{L}\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\leq C\epsilon t^{-\frac{3}{2}},

we have

(6.4)

\left\lVert\int\frac{(D_{t}\zeta)_{L}(\alpha)-(D_{t}\zeta)_{L}(\beta)}{\zeta(\alpha)-\zeta(\beta)}L_{0}(\bar{\zeta}_{\beta}-1)d\beta\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\leq C\epsilon t^{-1}\left\lVert L_{0}(\bar{\zeta}_{\alpha}-1)\right\rVert_{L^{2}}.

For the high frequency part (denoted by
I_{h}
), we write

\left\lVert D_{t}\partial_{\alpha}^{s}fI_{h}\right\rVert_{L^{2}(|\alpha|\leq t)}\leq\max\left\{\left\lVert D_{t}\partial_{\alpha}^{s}fI_{h}\right\rVert_{L^{2}(t^{3/4}\leq|\alpha|\leq t)},\ \left\lVert D_{t}\partial_{\alpha}^{s}fI_{h}\right\rVert_{L^{2}(|\alpha|\leq t^{3/4})}\right\}.

Note that

\displaystyle\left\lVert D_{t}\partial_{\alpha}^{s}fI_{h}\right\rVert_{L^{2}(|\alpha|\leq t^{3/4})}

\displaystyle\leq\left\lVert D_{t}\partial_{\alpha}^{s}f\int_{|\alpha-\beta|\leq 2t^{\frac{3}{4}}}\frac{(D_{t}\zeta)_{H}(\alpha)-(D_{t}\zeta)_{H}(\beta)}{\zeta(\alpha)-\zeta(\beta)}L_{0}(\bar{\zeta}_{\beta}-1)d\beta\right\rVert_{L^{2}(|\alpha|\leq t^{\frac{3}{4}})}

\displaystyle\ \ \ +\left\lVert D_{t}\partial_{\alpha}^{s}f(D_{t}\zeta)_{H}\int_{|\alpha-\beta|>2t^{3/4}}\frac{L_{0}(\bar{\zeta}_{\beta}-1)}{\zeta(\alpha)-\zeta(\beta)}d\beta\right\rVert_{L^{2}(|\alpha|\leq t^{3/4})}

\displaystyle\ \ \ +\left\lVert D_{t}\partial_{\alpha}^{s}f\int_{|\alpha-\beta|\geq 2t^{3/4}}\frac{L_{0}(\bar{\zeta}_{\beta}-1)}{\zeta(\alpha)-\zeta(\beta)}(D_{t}\zeta)_{H}(\beta)d\beta\right\rVert_{L^{2}(|\alpha|\leq t^{3/4})}.

For the first term, we have

(D_{t}\zeta)_{H}(\alpha)=\frac{4\alpha^{2}}{it^{2}}\partial_{\alpha}(D_{t}\zeta)_{H}-\frac{4\alpha}{it^{2}}L_{0}(D_{t}\zeta)_{H}+\frac{2}{it}\Omega_{0}(D_{t}\zeta)_{H}

which implies (use (
4.9
))

\left\lVert(D_{t}\zeta)_{H}\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(|\alpha|\leq Ct^{\frac{3}{4}})}\leq C\epsilon t^{-1+\delta_{0}}\ln t

and we derive (note that
|\alpha-\beta|\leq 2t^{\frac{3}{4}}
implies
|\beta|\leq 3t^{\frac{3}{4}}
)

\displaystyle\left\lVert D_{t}\partial_{\alpha}^{s}f\int_{|\alpha-\beta|\leq 2t^{\frac{3}{4}}}\frac{(D_{t}\zeta)_{H}(\alpha)-(D_{t}\zeta)_{H}(\beta)}{\zeta(\alpha)-\zeta(\beta)}L_{0}(\bar{\zeta}_{\beta}-1)d\beta\right\rVert_{L^{2}(|\alpha|\leq t^{\frac{3}{4}})}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\ C\left\lVert D_{t}\partial_{\alpha}^{s}f\right\rVert_{L^{2}}\left\lVert\int_{|\alpha-\beta|\leq 2t^{\frac{3}{4}}}\frac{(D_{t}\zeta)_{H}(\alpha)-(D_{t}\zeta)_{H}(\beta)}{\zeta(\alpha)-\zeta(\beta)}L_{0}(\bar{\zeta}_{\beta}-1)d\beta\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(|\alpha|\leq t^{\frac{3}{4}})}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\ C\epsilon\left\lVert\int_{|\alpha-\beta|\leq 1}\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(|\alpha|\leq t^{\frac{3}{4}})}+C\epsilon\left\lVert\int_{1<|\alpha-\beta|\leq t^{\frac{3}{4}}}\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(|\alpha|\leq t^{\frac{3}{4}})}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\ C\epsilon\left\lVert\partial_{\alpha}(D_{t}\zeta)_{H}\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(|\alpha|\leq 3t^{\frac{3}{4}})}\left\lVert L_{0}(\bar{\zeta}_{\alpha}-1)\right\rVert_{L^{2}}+C\epsilon\left\lVert(D_{t}\zeta)_{H}\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(|\alpha|\leq 3t^{\frac{3}{4}})}\int_{|\alpha-\beta|\geq 1}\left|\frac{L_{0}(\bar{\zeta}_{\alpha}-1)}{\zeta(\alpha)-\zeta(\beta)}\right|d\beta

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\ C\epsilon^{2}t^{-1+\delta_{0}}\ln t\left\lVert L_{0}(\bar{\zeta}_{\alpha}-1)\right\rVert_{L^{2}}.

For the second term, there holds

\displaystyle\left\lVert D_{t}\partial_{\alpha}^{s}f(D_{t}\zeta)_{H}\int_{|\alpha-\beta|\geq 2t^{\frac{3}{4}}}\frac{L_{0}(\bar{\zeta}_{\beta}-1)}{\zeta(\alpha)-\zeta(\beta)}d\beta\right\rVert_{L^{2}(|\alpha|\leq t^{\frac{3}{4}})}

\displaystyle\leq C\epsilon^{2}t^{-1+\delta_{0}}\ln t\times t^{-\frac{3}{8}}\left\lVert L_{0}(\bar{\zeta}_{\alpha}-1)\right\rVert_{L^{2}},

\displaystyle\leq C\epsilon^{3}t^{-1-\delta}.

For the third term, note that
|\alpha-\beta|\geq 2t^{\frac{3}{4}}\geq 2|\alpha|
, which implies
C|\alpha-\beta|\geq|\beta|\geq t^{\frac{3}{4}}
and thus

(6.5)

\displaystyle\left\lVert D_{t}\partial_{\alpha}^{s}f\int_{|\alpha-\beta|\geq 2t^{\frac{3}{4}}}\frac{L_{0}(\bar{\zeta}_{\beta}-1)}{\zeta(\alpha)-\zeta(\beta)}(D_{t}\zeta)_{H}(\beta)d\beta\right\rVert_{L^{2}(|\alpha|\leq t^{\frac{3}{4}})}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\epsilon\left\lVert\int_{\begin{subarray}{c}|\alpha-\beta|\geq 2t^{\frac{3}{4}},\\
|\beta|\geq t^{\frac{3}{4}}\end{subarray}}\left|\frac{L_{0}(\bar{\zeta}_{\beta}-1)}{\zeta(\alpha)-\zeta(\beta)}(D_{t}\zeta)_{H}(\beta)\right|d\beta\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(|\alpha|\leq t^{\frac{3}{4}})},

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\epsilon t^{-1}\int_{|\beta|\geq t^{\frac{3}{4}}}\left|\frac{t}{\beta}(D_{t}\zeta)_{H}(\beta)\right||L_{0}(\bar{\zeta}_{\beta}-1)|d\beta,

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\epsilon t^{-1}\int_{|\beta|\geq t^{\frac{3}{4}}}\left(|\partial_{t}(D_{t}\zeta)_{H}|+\frac{1}{|\beta|}|\Omega_{0}(D_{t}\zeta)_{H}|\right)|L_{0}(\bar{\zeta}_{\beta}-1)|d\beta,

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\epsilon^{2}t^{-1}\left\lVert L_{0}(\bar{\zeta}_{\alpha}-1)\right\rVert_{L^{2}}.

So we conclude that

(6.6)

\left\lVert D_{t}\partial_{\alpha}^{s}fI_{h}\right\rVert_{L^{2}(|\alpha|\leq t^{\frac{3}{4}})}\leq C\epsilon^{2}t^{-1+\delta_{0}}\ln t\left\lVert L_{0}(\bar{\zeta}_{\alpha}-1)\right\rVert_{L^{2}}.

For
t^{3/4}<|\alpha|\leq t
, define
E_{t}=\left\{\alpha\in\mathbb{R}\ |\ t^{3/4}<|\alpha|\leq t\right\}
. Still, we decompose the integral as

\displaystyle\left\lVert D_{t}\partial_{\alpha}^{s}fI_{h}\right\rVert_{L^{2}(E_{t})}

\displaystyle\leq\left\lVert D_{t}\partial_{\alpha}^{s}f\int_{|\alpha-\beta|\leq\frac{1}{2}t^{3/4}}\frac{(D_{t}\zeta)_{H}(\alpha)-(D_{t}\zeta)_{H}(\beta)}{\zeta(\alpha)-\zeta(\beta)}L_{0}(\bar{\zeta}_{\beta}-1)d\beta\right\rVert_{L^{2}(E_{t})}

\displaystyle\ \ \ +\left\lVert D_{t}\partial_{\alpha}^{s}f(D_{t}\zeta)_{H}\int_{|\alpha-\beta|\geq\frac{1}{2}t^{3/4}}\frac{L_{0}(\bar{\zeta}_{\beta}-1)}{\zeta(\alpha)-\zeta(\beta)}d\beta\right\rVert_{L^{2}(E_{t})}

\displaystyle\ \ \ +\left\lVert D_{t}\partial_{\alpha}^{s}f\int_{|\alpha-\beta|\geq\frac{1}{2}t^{3/4}}\frac{L_{0}(\bar{\zeta}_{\beta}-1)}{\zeta(\alpha)-\zeta(\beta)}(D_{t}\zeta)_{H}(\beta)d\beta\right\rVert_{L^{2}(E_{t})}.

For the first term, observe that
|\alpha-\beta|\leq\frac{1}{2}t^{3/4}
and
\alpha\in E(t)
implies
|\alpha|/2\leq|\beta|\leq 2|\alpha|
. So applying Lemma
4.12
yields the result (note that we can transit the derivative on
E_{t}
). Also, the second term behaves well for a similar reason. For the third term, we decompose it as

\displaystyle\int_{|\alpha-\beta|\geq\frac{1}{2}t^{3/4}}\frac{L_{0}(\bar{\zeta}_{\beta}-1)}{\zeta(\alpha)-\zeta(\beta)}(D_{t}\zeta)_{H}(\beta)d\beta

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left(\int_{\begin{subarray}{c}|\alpha-\beta|\geq\frac{1}{2}t^{3/4}\\
|\beta|\leq|\alpha|/2\end{subarray}}+\int_{\begin{subarray}{c}|\alpha-\beta|\geq\frac{1}{2}t^{3/4}\\
|\alpha|/2<|\beta|\leq 2|\alpha|\end{subarray}}+\int_{\begin{subarray}{c}|\alpha-\beta|\geq\frac{1}{2}t^{3/4}\\
|\beta|>2|\alpha|\end{subarray}}\right)\frac{L_{0}(\bar{\zeta}_{\beta}-1)}{\zeta(\alpha)-\zeta(\beta)}(D_{t}\zeta)_{H}(\beta)d\beta,

\displaystyle\overset{\text{def}}{=}

\displaystyle I_{1}+I_{2}+I_{3}.

For
I_{1}
, we have

\displaystyle\left\lVert I_{1}\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(E_{t})}

\displaystyle\leq\left\lVert\int_{|\beta|\leq|\alpha|/2}\left|\frac{L_{0}(\bar{\zeta}_{\beta}-1)}{\zeta(\alpha)-\zeta(\beta)}(D_{t}\zeta)_{H}(\beta)\right|d\beta\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(E_{t})},

\displaystyle\leq C\left\lVert\int_{|\beta|\leq|\alpha|/2}\left|\frac{L_{0}(\bar{\zeta}_{\beta}-1)}{|\alpha|}\left(-\frac{2\beta}{it}\partial_{t}(D_{t}\zeta)_{H}+\frac{2}{it}\Omega_{0}(D_{t}\zeta)_{H}\right)\right|d\beta\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(E_{t})},

\displaystyle\leq Ct^{-1}\int_{|\beta|\leq t/2}|L_{0}(\bar{\zeta}_{\beta}-1)||\partial_{t}(D_{t}\zeta)_{H}|d\beta

\displaystyle\ \ \ +C\epsilon t^{-1+\delta_{0}}\ln t\left\lVert\frac{1}{|\alpha|}\int_{|\beta|\leq|\alpha|/2}|L_{0}(\bar{\zeta}_{\beta}-1)|d\beta\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(E_{t})},

\displaystyle\leq C\epsilon t^{-1}\left\lVert L_{0}(\bar{\zeta}_{\alpha}-1)\right\rVert_{L^{2}}+C\epsilon^{2}t^{-1-\frac{3}{8}+2\delta_{0}}\ln t

which is enough. For
I_{2}
, note that
|\beta|<2|\alpha|
implies
|\alpha-\beta|<3|\alpha|\leq 3t
and we have

\displaystyle\left\lVert D_{t}\partial_{\alpha}^{s}fI_{2}\right\rVert_{L^{2}(E_{t})}

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{|\alpha|/2\leq|\beta|\leq 2|\alpha|}|(D_{t}\zeta)_{H}(\beta)D_{t}\partial_{\alpha}^{s}f|\left\lVert\int_{\begin{subarray}{c}|\alpha-\beta|>\frac{1}{2}t^{3/4}\\
|\alpha|/2<|\beta|\leq 2|\alpha|\end{subarray}}\left|\frac{L_{0}(\bar{\zeta}_{\beta}-1)}{\alpha-\beta}\right|\left|\frac{\alpha-\beta}{\zeta(\alpha)-\zeta(\beta)}\right|d\beta\right\rVert_{L^{2}(E_{t})},

\displaystyle\leq C\epsilon^{2}t^{-1}\left\lVert\int_{\frac{1}{2}t^{3/4}<|\alpha-\beta|\leq 3t}\left|\frac{L_{0}(\bar{\zeta}_{\beta}-1)}{\alpha-\beta}\right|d\beta\right\rVert_{L^{2}},

\displaystyle\leq C\epsilon^{2}t^{-1}\left\lVert\chi_{\frac{1}{2}t^{3/4}\leq|\alpha|\leq 3t}\frac{1}{|\alpha|}\right\rVert_{L^{1}}\left\lVert L_{0}(\bar{\zeta}_{\alpha}-1)\right\rVert_{L^{2}},

\displaystyle\leq C\epsilon^{2}t^{-1}\ln t\left\lVert L_{0}(\bar{\zeta}_{\alpha}-1)\right\rVert_{L^{2}}.

For
I_{3}
, note that
|\alpha-\beta|\geq|\beta|/2
and we thus have

\displaystyle\left\lVert I_{3}\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(E_{t})}\leq C\int_{|\beta|>2|\alpha|>2t^{3/4}}\left|\frac{L_{0}(\bar{\zeta}_{\beta}-1)}{\beta}(D_{t}\zeta)_{H}(\beta)\right|d\beta\leq C\epsilon t^{-1}\left\lVert L_{0}(\bar{\zeta}_{\alpha}-1)\right\rVert_{L^{2}}.

Here we have used the same estimate in (
6.5
). So we conclude that

\left\lVert D_{t}\partial_{\alpha}^{s}fI_{h}\right\rVert_{L^{2}(|\alpha|\leq t)}\leq C\epsilon^{2}t^{-1+\delta_{0}}\ln t\left\lVert L_{0}(\bar{\zeta}_{\alpha}-1)\right\rVert_{L^{2}}+C\epsilon^{3}t^{-1-\delta}.

Then we consider II. For
\alpha\in E_{t}
, write

\displaystyle\left\lVert D_{t}\partial_{\alpha}^{s}fII\right\rVert_{L^{2}(E_{t})}

\displaystyle\leq\left\lVert D_{t}\partial_{\alpha}^{s}fL_{0}D_{t}\zeta(\alpha)\frac{\bar{\zeta}_{\alpha}-1}{\zeta_{\alpha}}\right\rVert_{L^{2}(E_{t})}+\left\lVert D_{t}\partial_{\alpha}^{s}f\int\frac{L_{0}D_{t}\zeta(\beta)}{\zeta(\alpha)-\zeta(\beta)}(\bar{\zeta}_{\beta}-1)d\beta\right\rVert_{L^{2}(E_{t})},

\displaystyle\leq C\epsilon^{2}t^{-1}\left\lVert L_{0}D_{t}\zeta\right\rVert_{L^{2}}+\left\lVert D_{t}\partial_{\alpha}^{s}f\int\frac{L_{0}D_{t}\zeta(\beta)}{\zeta(\alpha)-\zeta(\beta)}(\bar{\zeta}_{\beta}-1)d\beta\right\rVert_{L^{2}(E_{t})}

and the second term is treated by the same method which is applied to
\left\lVert D_{t}\partial_{\alpha}^{s}fI_{h}\right\rVert_{L^{2}(E_{t})}
5
5

5

Namely, decompose the integral into two parts:
|\alpha-\beta|\leq\frac{1}{2}t^{3/4}
and
|\alpha-\beta|>\frac{1}{2}t^{3/4}
. For the former, apply Lemma
4.12
; for the latter, apply the same argument in (
6.5
).

For
|\alpha|\leq t^{\frac{3}{4}}
, we have

\displaystyle\left\lVert D_{t}\partial_{\alpha}^{s}fII\right\rVert_{L^{2}(|\alpha|\leq t^{\frac{3}{4}})}

\displaystyle\leq\left\lVert D_{t}\partial_{\alpha}^{s}f\int_{|\zeta(\alpha)-\zeta(\beta)|\leq 1}\frac{L_{0}D_{t}\zeta(\alpha)-L_{0}D_{t}\zeta(\beta)}{\zeta(\alpha)-\zeta(\beta)}(\bar{\zeta}_{\beta}-1)d\beta\right\rVert_{L^{2}(|\alpha|\leq t^{\frac{3}{4}})}

\displaystyle\ \ \ +\left\lVert D_{t}\partial_{\alpha}^{s}f\int_{1<|\zeta(\alpha)-\zeta(\beta)|\leq 2t^{3/4}}\frac{L_{0}D_{t}\zeta(\alpha)-L_{0}D_{t}\zeta(\beta)}{\zeta(\alpha)-\zeta(\beta)}(\bar{\zeta}_{\beta}-1)d\beta\right\rVert_{L^{2}(|\alpha|\leq t^{\frac{3}{4}})}

\displaystyle\ \ \ +\left\lVert D_{t}\partial_{\alpha}^{s}f\int_{|\zeta(\alpha)-\zeta(\beta)|\geq 2t^{3/4}}\frac{L_{0}D_{t}\zeta(\alpha)-L_{0}D_{t}\zeta(\beta)}{\zeta(\alpha)-\zeta(\beta)}(\bar{\zeta}_{\beta}-1)d\beta\right\rVert_{L^{2}(|\alpha|\leq t^{\frac{3}{4}})},

\displaystyle\overset{\text{def}}{=}II_{1}+II_{2}+II_{3}.

Note that

|\zeta(\alpha)-\zeta(\beta)|\leq Ct^{3/4},|\alpha|\leq t^{3/4}\Rightarrow|\beta|\leq|\alpha|+\left|\frac{\alpha-\beta}{\zeta(\alpha)-\zeta(\beta)}\right||\zeta(\alpha)-\zeta(\beta)|\leq Ct^{3/4}

and

\left\lVert\bar{\zeta}_{\alpha}-1\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(|\alpha|\leq t^{\frac{3}{4}})}\leq Ct^{-1}\left\lVert\tilde{\Omega}_{0}(\bar{\zeta}_{\alpha}-1)\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}+O(\epsilon t^{-1})\leq C\epsilon t^{-1+\delta_{0}}.

At first, we use Hardy’s inequality to get

II_{1}\leq C\epsilon\left\lVert\partial_{\alpha}L_{0}D_{t}\zeta\right\rVert_{L^{2}}\left\lVert\bar{\zeta}_{\alpha}-1\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(|\alpha|\leq Ct^{\frac{3}{4}})}\leq C\epsilon^{2}t^{-1+\delta_{0}}\left\lVert\partial_{\alpha}L_{0}D_{t}\zeta\right\rVert_{L^{2}};

then, observe that

II_{2}\leq C\epsilon\left\lVert L_{0}D_{t}\zeta\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\left\lVert\bar{\zeta}_{\alpha}-1\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(|\alpha|\leq Ct^{\frac{3}{4}})}\ln t\leq C\epsilon^{2}t^{-1+\delta_{0}}\ln t\left\lVert L_{0}D_{t}\zeta\right\rVert_{H^{1}}.

For
II_{3}
, we split
L_{0}D_{t}\zeta(\alpha)-L_{0}D_{t}\zeta(\beta)
into two parts, and

D_{t}\partial_{\alpha}^{s}f\int_{|\zeta(\alpha)-\zeta(\beta)|\geq 2t^{3/4}}\frac{L_{0}D_{t}\zeta(\beta)}{\zeta(\alpha)-\zeta(\beta)}(\bar{\zeta}_{\beta}-1)d\beta

is treated by the argument for (
6.5
). Finally, we deduce that

\displaystyle\left\lVert D_{t}\partial_{\alpha}^{s}fL_{0}D_{t}\zeta\int_{|\zeta(\alpha)-\zeta(\beta)|\geq 2t^{3/4}}\frac{\bar{\zeta}_{\beta}-1}{\zeta(\alpha)-\zeta(\beta)}d\beta\right\rVert_{L^{2}(|\alpha|\leq t^{\frac{3}{4}})}

\displaystyle\leq C\epsilon\left\lVert L_{0}D_{t}\zeta\right\rVert_{H^{1}}\left\lVert\int_{|\zeta(\alpha)-\zeta(\beta)|\geq 2t^{3/4}}\frac{\bar{\zeta}_{\beta}-1}{\zeta(\alpha)-\zeta(\beta)}d\beta\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(|\alpha|\leq t^{\frac{3}{4}})},

\displaystyle\leq C\epsilon\left\lVert L_{0}D_{t}\zeta\right\rVert_{H^{1}}\left\lVert\frac{\bar{\zeta}_{\alpha}-1}{\zeta_{\alpha}}\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(|\alpha|\leq t^{\frac{3}{4}})}+C\epsilon\left\lVert L_{0}D_{t}\zeta\right\rVert_{H^{1}}\left\lVert\int_{|\zeta(\alpha)-\zeta(\beta)|\leq 2t^{3/4}}\frac{\bar{\zeta}_{\beta}-1}{\zeta(\alpha)-\zeta(\beta)}d\beta\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(|\alpha|\leq t^{\frac{3}{4}})},

\displaystyle\leq C\epsilon^{2}t^{-1+\delta_{0}}\ln t\left\lVert L_{0}D_{t}\zeta\right\rVert_{H^{1}}.

Therefore, we conclude that
\left\lVert D_{t}\partial_{\alpha}^{s}fII\right\rVert_{L^{2}(|\alpha|\leq t)}\leq C\epsilon^{2}t^{-1+\delta_{0}}\ln t\left\lVert L_{0}D_{t}\zeta\right\rVert_{H^{1}}+O(\epsilon^{3}t^{-1-\delta})
. Due to the same reason that is stated in the proof for Lemma
6.1
, we omit the estimate for III and point out that

\left\lVert(I-\mathcal{H})bD_{t}\partial_{\alpha}^{s}f\right\rVert_{L^{2}(R)}\leq C\epsilon^{4}t^{-1}

without proof. This completes the proof.
∎

Corollary 6.1
.

Under the assumption of Lemma
6.1
, there holds

\left\lVert[L_{0},D_{t}^{2}-iA\partial_{\alpha}]\partial_{\alpha}^{k}f\right\rVert_{L^{2}(R)}\leq C\epsilon^{2}t^{-1}(\left\lVert L_{0}(\zeta_{\alpha}-1)\right\rVert_{L^{2}}+\left\lVert L_{0}D_{t}\zeta\right\rVert_{L^{2}}+\left\lVert L_{0}(\zeta-\alpha)\right\rVert_{\dot{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}})+C\epsilon^{3}t^{-1}

for
0\leq k\leq s-2
.

Proof.

According to Remark
6.1
, we shall focus on
|\alpha|\leq t
. Still, we only consider the term
L_{0}bD_{t}\partial_{\alpha}\partial_{\alpha}^{k}f
. At first, thanks to Lemma
4.2
, we have
\left\lVert D_{t}\partial_{\alpha}^{k+1}f\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\leq C\epsilon t^{-1/6}\ln t
.
Then, we list out the estimate for each term of
D_{t}\partial_{\alpha}^{k+1}fI
which does not decay with speed
t^{-1}
in
L^{2}
in the proof of Proposition
6.1
.

(1)

\displaystyle\left\lVert D_{t}\partial_{\alpha}^{k+1}f\int_{|\alpha-\beta|\leq 2t^{\frac{3}{4}}}\frac{(D_{t}\zeta)_{H}(\alpha)-(D_{t}\zeta)_{H}(\beta)}{\zeta(\alpha)-\zeta(\beta)}L_{0}(\bar{\zeta}_{\beta}-1)d\beta\right\rVert_{L^{2}(|\alpha|\leq t^{\frac{3}{4}})}

\displaystyle\leq C\left\lVert D_{t}\partial_{\alpha}^{k+1}f\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\left\lVert(D_{t}\zeta)_{H}\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(|\alpha|\leq 3t^{3/4})}\left\lVert L_{0}(\bar{\zeta}_{\alpha}-1)\right\rVert_{L^{2}},

\displaystyle\leq C\epsilon^{3}t^{-\frac{1}{6}-1+\delta_{0}}(\ln t)^{2},

\displaystyle\leq C\epsilon^{3}t^{-1-\delta}.

(2)

\displaystyle\left\lVert D_{t}\partial_{\alpha}^{k+1}f\int_{\begin{subarray}{c}|\alpha-\beta|>\frac{1}{2}t^{3/4}\\
|\alpha|/2<|\beta|\leq 2|\alpha|\end{subarray}}\frac{L_{0}(\bar{\zeta}_{\beta}-1)}{\zeta(\alpha)-\zeta(\beta)}(D_{t}\zeta)_{H}(\beta)d\beta\right\rVert_{L^{2}(E_{t})}

\displaystyle\leq\left\lVert D_{t}\partial_{\alpha}^{k+1}f\int_{\begin{subarray}{c}\frac{1}{2}t^{3/4}<|\alpha-\beta|\leq\frac{1}{2}t^{6/7}\\
|\alpha|/2<|\beta|\leq 2|\alpha|\end{subarray}}\frac{L_{0}(\bar{\zeta}_{\beta}-1)}{\zeta(\alpha)-\zeta(\beta)}(D_{t}\zeta)_{H}(\beta)d\beta\right\rVert_{L^{2}(E_{t})}

\displaystyle\ \ \ +\left\lVert D_{t}\partial_{\alpha}^{k+1}f\int_{\begin{subarray}{c}|\alpha-\beta|>\frac{1}{2}t^{6/7}\\
|\alpha|/2<|\beta|\leq 2|\alpha|\end{subarray}}\frac{L_{0}(\bar{\zeta}_{\beta}-1)}{\zeta(\alpha)-\zeta(\beta)}(D_{t}\zeta)_{H}(\beta)d\beta\right\rVert_{L^{2}(E_{t})},

\displaystyle\leq\left\lVert D_{t}\partial_{\alpha}^{k+1}f\int_{\begin{subarray}{c}\frac{1}{2}t^{3/4}<|\alpha-\beta|\leq\frac{1}{2}t^{6/7}\\
|\alpha|/2<|\beta|\leq 2|\alpha|\end{subarray}}\frac{L_{0}(\bar{\zeta}_{\beta}-1)}{\zeta(\alpha)-\zeta(\beta)}(D_{t}\zeta)_{H}(\beta)d\beta\right\rVert_{L^{2}(E_{t})}

\displaystyle\ \ \ +C\left\lVert D_{t}\partial_{\alpha}^{k+1}f\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}t^{-6/7}\left\lVert(D_{t}\zeta)_{H}\right\rVert_{L^{2}}\left\lVert L_{0}(\bar{\zeta}_{\alpha}-1)\right\rVert_{L^{2}},

\displaystyle\leq\left\lVert D_{t}\partial_{\alpha}^{k+1}f\int_{\begin{subarray}{c}\frac{1}{2}t^{3/4}<|\alpha-\beta|\leq\frac{1}{2}t^{6/7}\\
|\alpha|/2<|\beta|\leq 2|\alpha|\end{subarray}}\frac{L_{0}(\bar{\zeta}_{\beta}-1)}{\zeta(\alpha)-\zeta(\beta)}(D_{t}\zeta)_{H}(\beta)d\beta\right\rVert_{L^{2}(E_{t})}+C\epsilon^{3}t^{-1-\frac{1}{42}+\delta_{0}}\ln t.

Denote the integral by
\tilde{I}
and we have

\displaystyle\left\lVert D_{t}\partial_{\alpha}^{k+1}f\tilde{I}\right\rVert_{L^{2}(t^{3/4}<|\alpha|\leq t^{6/7})}

\displaystyle\leq\left\lVert D_{t}\partial_{\alpha}^{k+1}f\right\rVert_{L^{2}}\left\lVert(D_{t}\zeta)_{H}\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}(|\alpha|\leq 2t^{6/7})}\int_{|\alpha-\beta|\geq\frac{1}{2}t^{3/4}}\left|\frac{L_{0}(\bar{\zeta}_{\beta}-1)}{\zeta(\alpha)-\zeta(\beta)}\right|d\beta,

\displaystyle\leq C\epsilon^{2}t^{-\frac{1}{2}-\frac{1}{7}}t^{-\frac{3}{8}}\left\lVert L_{0}(\bar{\zeta}_{\alpha}-1)\right\rVert_{L^{2}},

\displaystyle\leq C\epsilon^{3}t^{-1-\frac{1}{42}+\delta_{0}}.

Finally, since

|\alpha|>t^{6/7},|\alpha-\beta|\leq\frac{1}{2}t^{6/7}\Rightarrow|\alpha-\beta|<\frac{|\alpha|}{2}\Rightarrow\frac{|\alpha|}{2}\leq|\beta|\leq 2|\alpha|,

we have

1_{|\alpha|>t^{6/7}}\tilde{I}=\int_{\frac{1}{2}t^{3/4}<|\alpha-\beta|\leq\frac{1}{2}t^{6/7}}\frac{L_{0}(\bar{\zeta}_{\beta}-1)}{\zeta(\alpha)-\zeta(\beta)}(D_{t}\zeta)_{H}(\beta)d\beta

which allows us to apply T1 Theorem:

\displaystyle\left\lVert D_{t}\partial_{\alpha}^{k+1}f\tilde{I}\right\rVert_{L^{2}(t^{6/7}<|\alpha|\leq t)}

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{|\alpha|/2\leq|\beta|\leq 2|\alpha|}|D_{t}\partial_{\alpha}^{k+1}f(D_{t}\zeta)_{H}(\beta)|\left\lVert L_{0}(\bar{\zeta}_{\alpha}-1)\right\rVert_{L^{2}},

\displaystyle\leq C\epsilon^{2}t^{-1}\left\lVert L_{0}(\bar{\zeta}_{\alpha}-1)\right\rVert_{L^{2}}.

And we conclude that

\left\lVert D_{t}\partial_{\alpha}^{k+1}f\int_{\begin{subarray}{c}|\alpha-\beta|>\frac{1}{2}t^{3/4}\\
|\alpha|/2<|\beta|\leq 2|\alpha|\end{subarray}}\frac{L_{0}(\bar{\zeta}_{\beta}-1)}{\zeta(\alpha)-\zeta(\beta)}(D_{t}\zeta)_{H}(\beta)d\beta\right\rVert_{L^{2}(E_{t})}\leq C\epsilon^{2}t^{-1}\left\lVert L_{0}(\bar{\zeta}_{\alpha}-1)\right\rVert_{L^{2}}+C\epsilon^{3}t^{-1-\delta}.

(3)

\displaystyle II_{1},II_{2},II_{3}\leq\left\lVert D_{t}\partial_{\alpha}^{k+1}f\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\left\lVert L_{0}D_{t}\zeta\right\rVert_{L^{2}}\left\lVert\bar{\zeta}_{\alpha}-1\right\rVert_{W^{1,\infty}(|\alpha|\leq t^{\frac{3}{4}})}\ln t\leq C\epsilon^{3}t^{-1-\delta}.

This completes the proof.
∎

Theorem 6.2
.

Set

E_{L_{0}\theta}^{k}(t)=\int\frac{|D_{t}L_{0}\theta_{k}|^{2}}{A}d\alpha+i\int L_{0}\theta_{k}\partial_{\alpha}L_{0}\bar{\theta}_{k}d\alpha,

E_{L_{0}\sigma}^{k}(t)=\int\frac{|D_{t}L_{0}\sigma_{k}|^{2}}{A}d\alpha+i\int L_{0}\sigma_{k}\partial_{\alpha}L_{0}\bar{\sigma}_{k}d\alpha

and

\mathcal{E}_{k}^{L_{0}}(t)=\sum_{j=0}^{k}(E_{L_{0}\theta}^{j}(t)+E_{L_{0}\sigma}^{j}(t))

for
0\leq k\leq s-1
,
then

(1)

\displaystyle\left\lVert L_{0}(\zeta_{\alpha}-1)\right\rVert_{H^{k}}+\left\lVert L_{0}D_{t}\zeta\right\rVert_{H^{k}}+\left\lVert L_{0}\Lambda(\zeta-\alpha)\right\rVert_{H^{k}}+\left\lVert L_{0}\Lambda D_{t}\zeta\right\rVert_{H^{k}}+\left\lVert L_{0}D_{t}^{2}\zeta\right\rVert_{H^{k}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\ C\sqrt{\mathcal{E}_{k}^{L_{0}}(t)}+C\epsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}t^{-\frac{1}{4}}\ln t,\ \forall\ 0\leq k\leq s-1.

(2)

(6.7)

\left|\frac{d}{dt}(E_{L_{0}\theta}^{k}(t)+E_{L_{0}\sigma}^{k}(t))\right|\leq C\epsilon^{2}t^{-1}\left(\mathcal{E}_{k}^{L_{0}}+\epsilon\sqrt{\mathcal{E}_{k}^{L_{0}}}\right),\ \forall 0\leq k\leq s-2.

(3)

(6.8)

\left|\frac{d}{dt}(E_{L_{0}\theta}^{s-1}+E_{L_{0}\sigma}^{s-1})(t)\right|\leq C\epsilon^{2}t^{-1}\left(\mathcal{E}_{s-1}^{L_{0}}+\epsilon\sqrt{\mathcal{E}_{s-1}^{L_{0}}}\right)+C\epsilon^{3}t^{-1+3\delta_{0}}\ln t\sqrt{\mathcal{E}_{0}^{L_{0}}}.

Proof.

(1) is verified by applying an argument similar to Lemma
5.2
.

For (
6.7
) we only prove the case
k=0
, since others are similar to it (except the treatment of
L_{0}[D_{t}^{2}-iA\partial_{\alpha},\partial_{\alpha}^{k}]\theta
, which will be shown in the proof for (
6.8
)).

We have

(D_{t}^{2}-iA\partial_{\alpha})\theta=G

and thus

(D_{t}^{2}-iA\partial_{\alpha})L_{0}\theta=L_{0}G+[D_{t}^{2}-iA\partial_{\alpha},L_{0}]\theta

which leads to

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}E_{L_{0}\theta}^{0}(t)

\displaystyle=2Re\left\{\int\frac{D_{t}L_{0}\bar{\theta}}{A}L_{0}Gd\alpha\right\}-\int\frac{|D_{t}L_{0}\theta|^{2}}{A}\left(\frac{a_{t}}{a}\circ\kappa^{-1}\right)d\alpha

\displaystyle\ \ \ +2Re\left\{\int\frac{D_{t}L_{0}\bar{\theta}}{A}[D_{t}^{2}-iA\partial_{\alpha},L_{0}]\theta d\alpha\right\}.

According to Corollary
6.1
, we have

\displaystyle\left|-\int\frac{|D_{t}L_{0}\theta|^{2}}{A}\left(\frac{a_{t}}{a}\circ\kappa^{-1}\right)d\alpha+2Re\left\{\int\frac{D_{t}L_{0}\bar{\theta}}{A}[D_{t}^{2}-iA\partial_{\alpha},L_{0}]\theta d\alpha\right\}\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\ C\epsilon^{2}t^{-1-\delta}\left\lVert D_{t}L_{0}\theta\right\rVert_{L^{2}}^{2}+2\left\lVert[D_{t}^{2}-iA\partial_{\alpha},L_{0}]\theta\right\rVert_{L^{2}}\left\lVert D_{t}L_{0}\bar{\theta}\right\rVert_{L^{2}},

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\ C\epsilon^{2}t^{-1}E_{L_{0}\theta}^{0}(t)+C\epsilon^{2}t^{-1}\mathcal{E}_{0}^{L_{0}}(t)+C\epsilon^{3}t^{-1}\sqrt{\mathcal{E}_{0}^{L_{0}}(t)},

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\ C\epsilon^{2}t^{-1}\left(\mathcal{E}_{0}^{L_{0}}(t)+\epsilon\sqrt{\mathcal{E}_{0}^{L_{0}}(t)}\right).

In addition, since each term of
G
is cubic, one may exploit the technique of calculating
L_{0}b
and the method used in Corollary
6.1
(together with Lemma
6.1
, since we also need to control for large
|\alpha|
) to obtain
\left\lVert L_{0}G\right\rVert_{L^{2}}\leq C\epsilon^{2}t^{-1}\sqrt{\mathcal{E}_{0}^{L_{0}}(t)}+O(\epsilon^{3}t^{-1})
; now we get

\displaystyle\left|\frac{d}{dt}E_{L_{0}\theta}^{0}(t)\right|

\displaystyle\leq C\left\lVert D_{t}L_{0}\theta\right\rVert_{L^{2}}\left\lVert L_{0}G\right\rVert_{L^{2}}+C\epsilon^{2}t^{-1}\left(\mathcal{E}_{0}^{L_{0}}(t)+\epsilon\sqrt{\mathcal{E}_{0}^{L_{0}}(t)}\right)),

\displaystyle\leq C^{\prime}\epsilon^{2}t^{-1}\left(\mathcal{E}_{0}^{L_{0}}(t)+\epsilon\sqrt{\mathcal{E}_{0}^{L_{0}}(t)}\right).

The treatment of
\frac{d}{dt}E^{0}_{L_{0}\sigma}(t)
is similar.

For (
6.8
), simply use Proposition
6.1
to get

\displaystyle\left\lVert[D_{t}^{2}-iA\partial_{\alpha},L_{0}]\theta_{s-1}\right\rVert_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq C\epsilon^{2}t^{-1+\delta_{0}}\ln t\sqrt{\mathcal{E}_{0}^{L_{0}}(t)}+C\epsilon^{3}t^{-1}

and use
\left\lVert D_{t}L_{0}\bar{\theta}_{s-1}\right\rVert_{L^{2}}\leq C\epsilon t^{2\delta_{0}}
to derive

\left|\int\frac{D_{t}L_{0}\bar{\theta}_{s-1}}{A}[D_{t}^{2}-iA\partial_{\alpha},L_{0}]\theta_{s-1}d\alpha\right|\leq C\epsilon^{3}t^{-1+3\delta_{0}}\ln t\sqrt{\mathcal{E}_{0}^{L_{0}}(t)}+C\epsilon^{3}t^{-1}\sqrt{\mathcal{E}_{s-1}^{L_{0}}(t)}.

Also, we may apply its technique to derive the same bound for
\left\lVert L_{0}\partial_{\alpha}^{s-1}G\right\rVert_{L^{2}}
. Hence we only need to consider

\int\frac{D_{t}L_{0}\bar{\theta}_{s-1}}{A}L_{0}[D_{t}^{2}-iA\partial_{\alpha},\partial_{\alpha}^{s-1}]\theta d\alpha.

Recall that

[A\partial_{\alpha},\partial_{\alpha}^{s-1}]=-\sum_{j=1}^{s-1}C_{j,k}\partial_{\alpha}^{j}A\partial_{\alpha}^{s-j},

where
C_{j,k}=k!/j!(k-j)!
, and thus

L_{0}[A\partial_{\alpha},\partial_{\alpha}^{s-1}]\theta=-\sum_{j=1}^{s-1}C_{j,k}\left((L_{0}\partial_{\alpha}^{j}A)\partial_{\alpha}^{s-j}\theta+\partial_{\alpha}^{j}A(L_{0}\partial_{\alpha}^{s-j}\theta)\right).

Combining with
\left\lVert L_{0}A\right\rVert_{H^{s}}\leq C\epsilon^{2}t^{-\frac{1}{2}}\sqrt{\mathcal{E}_{s-1}^{L_{0}}(t)}
(also, this requires some techniques to avoid the overflow, like we did before),
\left\lVert\theta_{\alpha}\right\rVert_{W^{s-2,\infty}}\leq C\epsilon t^{-\frac{1}{2}}
and
\left\lVert A-1\right\rVert_{W^{s-1,\infty}}\leq\left\lVert A-1\right\rVert_{H^{s}}\leq C\epsilon^{2}t^{-1+\delta_{0}}\ln t
(
Note that for
0\leq k\leq s-2
, there holds
\left\lVert A-1\right\rVert_{W^{k,\infty}}\leq C\epsilon^{2}t^{-1-\delta}
for certain
\delta>0
, which makes the estimate for
k
order vector field energy different from
s-1
order
) yields

\left|\int\frac{D_{t}L_{0}\bar{\theta}_{k}}{A}L_{0}[-iA\partial_{\alpha},\partial_{\alpha}^{s-1}]\theta d\alpha\right|\leq C\epsilon^{2}t^{-1}\mathcal{E}_{s-1}^{L_{0}}(t)+C\epsilon^{3}t^{-1+3\delta_{0}}\ln t\sqrt{\mathcal{E}^{L_{0}}_{0}}.

The last term comes from the estimate for the extreme term:

\displaystyle\left\lVert\partial_{\alpha}^{s-1}A(L_{0}\partial_{\alpha}\theta)\right\rVert_{L^{2}}\left\lVert D_{t}L_{0}\bar{\theta}_{s-1}\right\rVert_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\left\lVert A-1\right\rVert_{H^{s}}\left\lVert D_{t}L_{0}\bar{\theta}_{s-1}\right\rVert_{L^{2}}\sqrt{\mathcal{E}_{0}^{L_{0}}}

\displaystyle\leq C\epsilon^{3}t^{-1+3\delta_{0}}\ln t\sqrt{\mathcal{E}_{0}^{L_{0}}}.

A similar estimate works for the
[D_{t}^{2},\partial_{\alpha}^{s-1}]
part (recall that
\left\lVert b_{\alpha}\right\rVert_{W^{s-2,\infty}}\leq Ct^{-1-\delta}
), and this completes the proof.
∎

Corollary 6.2
.

There holds

(6.9)

\mathcal{E}_{k}^{L_{0}}(t)\leq\left[\left(\sqrt{\mathcal{E}_{k}^{L_{0}}(0)}+\epsilon\right)(1+t)^{\frac{C\epsilon^{2}}{2}}-\epsilon\right]^{2}\ \forall 0\leq k\leq s-2

and

(6.10)

\mathcal{E}_{s-1}^{L_{0}}(t)\leq(1+t)^{C\epsilon^{2}}\mathcal{E}_{s-1}^{L_{0}}(0)+\frac{C\epsilon^{4}}{3\delta_{0}-\frac{C\epsilon^{2}}{2}}(1+t)^{3\delta_{0}+\frac{C\epsilon^{2}}{2}}\ln(1+t)

Proof.

Note that for
t\in[0,1]
, there holds

\left|\frac{d}{dt}\mathcal{E}_{k}^{L_{0}}\right|\leq C\epsilon^{2}\mathcal{E}_{k}^{L_{0}},\ \forall k\leq s-2

since each relevant term is bounded. So we rewrite (
6.7
) as

\left|\frac{d}{dt}\mathcal{E}_{k}^{L_{0}}(t)\right|\leq C\epsilon^{2}(1+t)^{-1}\left(\mathcal{E}_{k}^{L_{0}}+\epsilon\sqrt{\mathcal{E}_{k}^{L_{0}}}\right)

to obtain

\frac{d\mathcal{E}_{k}^{L_{0}}}{\mathcal{E}_{k}^{L_{0}}+\epsilon\sqrt{\mathcal{E}_{k}^{L_{0}}}}\leq C\epsilon^{2}(1+t)^{-1}dt

and we integrate it over
[0,t]
to get

2\ln\frac{\sqrt{\mathcal{E}_{k}^{L_{0}}(t)}+\epsilon}{\sqrt{\mathcal{E}_{k}^{L_{0}}(0)}+\epsilon}\leq C\epsilon^{2}\ln(1+t)

which simplifies to (
6.9
). Plugging it into (
6.8
) (also, we replace
t
with
1+t
) yields

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}\mathcal{E}_{s-1}^{L_{0}}(t)

\displaystyle\leq C\epsilon^{2}(1+t)^{-1}\left(\mathcal{E}_{s-1}^{L_{0}}+\epsilon\sqrt{\mathcal{E}_{s-1}^{L_{0}}}\right)

\displaystyle\ \ \ +C\epsilon^{3}(1+t)^{-1+3\delta_{0}}\left[\left(\sqrt{\mathcal{E}_{k}^{L_{0}}(0)}+\epsilon\right)(1+t)^{\frac{C\epsilon^{2}}{2}}-\epsilon\right]\ln(1+t)

\displaystyle\leq C\epsilon^{2}(1+t)^{-1}\left(\mathcal{E}_{s-1}^{L_{0}}+\epsilon\sqrt{\mathcal{E}_{s-1}^{L_{0}}}\right)+C\epsilon^{4}(1+t)^{-1+3\delta_{0}+\frac{C\epsilon^{2}}{2}}\ln(1+t).

According to the bootstrap assumption,
\sqrt{E_{s-1}^{L_{0}}(t)}\leq C\epsilon t^{2\delta_{0}}
and the inequality simplifies to

\frac{d}{dt}\mathcal{E}_{s-1}^{L_{0}}(t)\leq C\epsilon^{2}(1+t)^{-1}\mathcal{E}_{s-1}^{L_{0}}(t)+C\epsilon^{4}(1+t)^{-1+3\delta_{0}+\frac{C\epsilon^{2}}{2}}\ln(1+t)

which is solvable. Indeed, set
s=-1+3\delta_{0}-\frac{C\epsilon^{2}}{2}
and one gets

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{s-1}^{L_{0}}(t)

\displaystyle\leq(1+t)^{C\epsilon^{2}}\mathcal{E}_{s-1}^{L_{0}}(0)+C\epsilon^{4}(1+t)^{C\epsilon^{2}}\int_{0}^{t}(1+t)^{s}\ln(1+t)dt

\displaystyle=(1+t)^{C\epsilon^{2}}\mathcal{E}_{s-1}^{L_{0}}(0)+C\epsilon^{4}(1+t)^{C\epsilon^{2}}\frac{(1+t)^{s+1}\ln(1+t)}{s+1}

\displaystyle\ \ \ -C\epsilon^{4}(1+t)^{C\epsilon^{2}}\left(\frac{(1+t)^{s+1}}{(s+1)^{2}}+\frac{1}{(s+1)^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq(1+t)^{C\epsilon^{2}}\mathcal{E}_{s-1}^{L_{0}}(0)+\frac{C\epsilon^{4}}{3\delta_{0}-\frac{C\epsilon^{2}}{2}}(1+t)^{3\delta_{0}+\frac{C\epsilon^{2}}{2}}\ln(1+t).

This completes the proof.
∎

Corollary 6.3
.

There holds

(6.11)

\left\lVert\Omega_{0}\partial_{\alpha}\theta\right\rVert_{H^{k}}+\left\lVert\Lambda\Omega_{0}Q_{\alpha}\right\rVert_{H^{k}}\leq C\epsilon(1+t)^{\frac{C\epsilon^{2}}{2}},\ \forall 0\leq k\leq s-2

and

(6.12)

\left\lVert\Omega_{0}\partial_{\alpha}\theta\right\rVert_{H^{s-1}}+\left\lVert\Lambda\Omega_{0}Q_{\alpha}\right\rVert_{H^{s-1}}\leq C\epsilon^{2}\sqrt{\frac{1}{3\delta_{0}-\frac{C\epsilon^{2}}{2}}}(1+t)^{\frac{3}{2}\delta_{0}+\frac{C\epsilon^{2}}{4}}\ln^{\frac{1}{2}}(1+t).

Proof.

Note that

(6.13)

\Omega_{0}\partial_{\alpha}=L_{0}\partial_{t}-\frac{t}{2}(\partial_{t}^{2}-i\partial_{\alpha})

which implies that for any
0\leq k\leq s-1
,

\left\lVert\Omega_{0}\partial_{\alpha}\theta\right\rVert_{H^{s-1}}\leq C\left\lVert L_{0}\partial_{t}\theta\right\rVert_{H^{s-1}}+\frac{t}{2}\left\lVert(\partial_{t}^{2}-i\partial_{\alpha})\theta\right\rVert_{H^{s-1}}.

For the second term, recall that
(D_{t}^{2}-iA\partial_{\alpha})\theta=G
is cubic, so

\displaystyle\left\lVert(\partial_{t}^{2}-i\partial_{\alpha})\theta\right\rVert_{H^{k}}\leq\left\lVert G\right\rVert_{H^{k}}+C\epsilon^{3}t^{-1}\leq C\epsilon^{3}t^{-1}.

For the first term, it’s enough for us to note that
L_{0}\partial_{t}=L_{0}(D_{t}-b\partial_{\alpha})
. Hence we have

\left\lVert\Omega_{0}\partial_{\alpha}\theta\right\rVert_{H^{k}}\leq C\sqrt{\mathcal{E}_{k}^{L_{0}}}(t)+C\epsilon^{3}\leq C\sqrt{\mathcal{E}_{k}^{L_{0}}(t)}

i.e.

\left\lVert\Omega_{0}\partial_{\alpha}\theta\right\rVert_{H^{k}}\leq\begin{cases}C\epsilon(1+t)^{\frac{C\epsilon^{2}}{2}},\ 0\leq k\leq s-2\\
\displaystyle C\epsilon^{2}\sqrt{\frac{1}{3\delta_{0}-\frac{C\epsilon^{2}}{2}}}(1+t)^{\frac{3}{2}\delta_{0}+\frac{C\epsilon^{2}}{4}}\ln^{\frac{1}{2}}(1+t),\ k=s-1.\end{cases}

Also, we may use formula (
6.13
) to derive

\left\lVert\Lambda\Omega_{0}Q_{\alpha}\right\rVert_{H^{k}}\leq\left\lVert\Lambda L_{0}\partial_{t}Q\right\rVert_{H^{k}}+\frac{t}{2}\left\lVert\Lambda(\partial_{t}^{2}-i\partial_{\alpha})Q\right\rVert_{H^{k}}.

For the first term, we write

\partial_{t}Q=D_{t}Q-b\partial_{\alpha}Q

and we shall only verify that
\left\lVert\Lambda L_{0}D_{t}Q\right\rVert_{L^{2}}+\frac{t}{2}\left\lVert\Lambda(\partial_{t}^{2}-i\partial_{\alpha})Q\right\rVert_{L^{2}}\leq C\sqrt{\mathcal{E}_{0}^{L_{0}}}
. Other terms or cases are similar to it. By utilizing (
2.11
), we have

(6.14)

L_{0}D_{t}Q=-L_{0}[D_{t}\zeta,\mathcal{H}]\frac{Re\left\{\bar{\zeta}_{\alpha}D_{t}\zeta\right\}}{\zeta_{\alpha}}+\frac{1}{2}L_{0}(I-\mathcal{H})|D_{t}\zeta|^{2}-\frac{1}{2i}L_{0}\theta.

For the first term, recall that

L_{0}[f,\mathcal{H}]\frac{g}{\zeta_{\alpha}}=[f,\mathcal{H}]\frac{L_{0}g}{\zeta_{\alpha}}+[L_{0}f,\mathcal{H}]\frac{g}{\zeta_{\alpha}}-\frac{1}{\pi i}\int\frac{(L_{0}\zeta(\alpha)-L_{0}\zeta(\beta))(f(\alpha)-f(\beta))}{(\zeta(\alpha)-\zeta(\beta))^{2}}gd\beta+[f,\mathcal{H}]\frac{g}{\zeta_{\alpha}}

and we have

\displaystyle\left\lVert L_{0}[D_{t}\zeta,\mathcal{H}]\frac{Re\left\{\bar{\zeta}_{\alpha}D_{t}\zeta\right\}}{\zeta_{\alpha}}\right\rVert_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq C\left\lVert D_{t}\zeta\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\left\lVert L_{0}Re\{\bar{\zeta}_{\alpha}D_{t}\zeta\}\right\rVert_{L^{2}}+C\left\lVert L_{0}D_{t}\zeta\right\rVert_{L^{2}}\left\lVert\mathcal{H}(\bar{\zeta}_{\alpha}D_{t}\zeta)\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\ \ \ +C\left\lVert D_{t}\zeta\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\left\lVert L_{0}(\zeta_{\alpha}-1)\right\rVert_{L^{2}}\left\lVert\bar{\zeta}_{\alpha}D_{t}\zeta\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}+\left\lVert D_{t}\zeta\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\left\lVert\bar{\zeta}_{\alpha}D_{t}\zeta\right\rVert_{L^{2}},

\displaystyle\leq C\epsilon\sqrt{\mathcal{E}_{0}^{L_{0}}}+C\epsilon^{2}.

Since

(I-\mathcal{H})|D_{t}\zeta|^{2}=(I-\mathcal{H})(D_{t}\bar{\zeta}D_{t}\zeta)=[\mathcal{H},D_{t}\zeta]D_{t}\bar{\zeta},

similar estimate works for the second term in (
6.14
) and their derivatives, hence we are allowed to use the embedding
\left\lVert\Lambda L_{0}f\right\rVert_{L^{2}}\leq\sqrt{\left\lVert L_{0}f\right\rVert_{L^{2}}\left\lVert\partial_{\alpha}L_{0}f\right\rVert_{L^{2}}}
while estimating the first and the second term of (
6.14
)
6
6

6

Such method doesn’t cause the overflow of the derivative. First, if all derivatives apply to a term that doesn’t involve vector field
L_{0}
, then the worst case is that there are
s+1/2
derivatives on
D_{t}\zeta
(after embedding), which is under control; second, if there are
s-1
derivatives apply to
L_{0}D_{t}\zeta
, then we shall use the bootstrap assumption
